RED kab: Agar premium ya Limit Order ka bhav range ke upar ya neeche ho, toh RED hota hai aur bot trade nahi leta. Range ke bahar ka Tagada Premium ho toh Range Alert jaata hai (dekho: Alerts aur insaan ki bhoomika).

Wajah: Bot ne aaj ke liye yahi range tay ki hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Target_Premium_Range`, `Entry_Order_Slippage_Buffer`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-24 | Med]` `[TAKRAAV #33]` Dikkat: `Target_Premium_Range` Delta band ke strikes aur free paise se banta hai. Isliye yeh Gate zyada tar Delta_Filter aur Balance_Ek_Lot_Gate ko hi dohrata hai. Range sirf `Mausam_Dobara_Jaanch_Samay` par banti hai, isliye beech mein bhav khiskne par purani range sahi strike ko rok sakti hai. Agar range ka kaam sirf paise ki seema hai, toh range har evaluation par taaze bhav se bane, aur Range Alert ka matlab ho "signal pass hua par paisa kam" (Sawal 130). Wajah: ek hi data se do Gate bina naye fayde ke trade na rokein.

### Balance_Ek_Lot_Gate

Naam: Balance_Ek_Lot_Gate. Dekhta hai: Broker account ka free paisa aur 1 Lot ko poora khareedne ka kharcha (premium ko `Ek_Lot_Quantity` se guna). GREEN kab: Agar free paisa 1 Lot ke poore kharche aur `Balance_Extra_Buffer` ke barabar ya zyada ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar free paisa utna na ho, toh RED hota hai aur bot trade nahi leta.

Wajah: Bot balance ko percentage risk mein nahi bandhta, sirf yeh dekhta hai ki kam se kam 1 Lot ka poora paisa hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Entry_Lot_Ginti`, `Balance_Extra_Buffer`, `Ek_Lot_Quantity`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-45 | Low]` Dikkat: kai Lot lene par `Balance_Extra_Buffer` har Lot par lagega ya ek baar, yeh saaf nahi. Agar bot kai Lot le, toh un sab Lot ka kharcha aur har Lot ka `Balance_Extra_Buffer` dono free paise ke andar hon; warna Lot ek-ek karke ghatein. Wajah: kharche ke baad order reject na ho.

### Expiry_Filter

Naam: Expiry_Filter. Dekhta hai: Index ki expiry ka din (Instrument Master se) aur ghadi ka samay. GREEN kab: Agar normal din current expiry ho ya expiry dopahar `Expiry_Shift_Aur_Exit_Samay` baad agli expiry ho, toh GREEN hota hai. BankNifty ke liye normal din mahine ka contract GREEN hai. RED kab: Agar expiry din `Expiry_Shift_Aur_Exit_Samay` baad current expiry ho ya bahut door ki expiry ho, toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: Expiry ki dopahar mein current contract ka theta tez galta hai aur door ki expiry nahi chalti. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Expiry_Shift_Aur_Exit_Samay`.

### IV_Overpriced_Filter

Naam: IV_Overpriced_Filter. Dekhta hai: Call aur Put strike ka alag IV (bid aur ask ke beech ke bhav se) aur index ki Realized Volatility (dekho: Tayyar Code (Repo)). GREEN kab: Agar IV khabar se pehle phoola na ho aur Realized Volatility ke `IV_Overpriced_Hadd` ke andar ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar khabar se pehle IV phool gaya ho ya `IV_Overpriced_Hadd` se upar ho, toh RED hota hai. NA kab: Agar bid-ask ka farak `IV_Spread_Hadd` se chauda ho, toh IV NA hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`.

Wajah: Phoola hua mehenga premium khabar ke baad IV Crush mein achanak gir jaata hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `IV_Overpriced_Hadd`, `IV_Spread_Hadd`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-42 | Med]` Realized Volatility `Realized_Volatility_Avadhi` ki avadhi se nikalti hai. "Khabar se pehle IV phool gaya" ka matlab hai: Bade_Event_Lock ki khabar se pehle ke din IV `IV_Overpriced_Hadd` se upar ho. Wajah: avadhi tay na ho toh yeh check har baar alag nateeja dega.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Hypothesis H-01: jab IV aur Realized Volatility ka anupaat kam ho aur IVP neeche ho, tab entry ki Net Expectancy zyada hoti hai. Test: Backtest mein trades ko IV-RV anupaat ke khaanon mein baant kar Net Expectancy milao. Wajah: sasta premium khareedna theta aur IV Crush ka nuksan ghatata hai.

### IVP_Filter

Naam: IVP_Filter. Dekhta hai: Pichle `IVP_Lookback_Din` ke usi tarah ki expiry aur bache dino wale IV se aaj ka IVP. Jab tak bot ka apna itna lamba IV record na ho, woh usi avadhi ka India VIX ka percentile use karta hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. GREEN kab: Agar IVP `IVP_Sasta_Hadd` se neeche ya `IVP_Medium_Range` mein ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar IVP `IVP_Mehenga_Hadd` se upar ho (sivay Shock Mode ke baad bade clean breakout ke), toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: Saal bhar ke mukable bahut mehenge IV mein khareedne par IV girne ka nuksan hota hai. Bharosa: pakka data (shuru mein VIX percentile andaza hai). Number: `IVP_Lookback_Din`, `IVP_Sasta_Hadd`, `IVP_Medium_Range`, `IVP_Mehenga_Hadd`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-51 | Med]` Dikkat: "Shock Mode ke baad bade clean breakout" wali chhoot ka koi paimana nahi hai. Agar malik yeh paimana tay na kare, toh pehle version mein yeh chhoot band rahe aur IVP `IVP_Mehenga_Hadd` se upar hone par Gate RED hi rahe. Wajah: vague chhoot se mehenge IV mein trade ho sakta hai. Asar: kuch bade din chhoot sakte hain, par IV Crush ka nuksan bachta hai.

### Liquidity_Filter

Naam: Liquidity_Filter. Dekhta hai: Broker ke `Market_Depth_Levels` data mein khareedte aur bechte waqt ke orders aur Slippage. GREEN kab: Agar khareedne aur bechne dono waqt rate ka farak `Liquidity_Slippage_Hadd` ke andar ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar rate ka farak `Liquidity_Slippage_Hadd` se zyada ho, toh "Low Liquidity" RED hota hai. 1 Lot ka order tukdon mein nahi bantta; depth kam ho toh strike chhoda jaata hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`.

Wajah: Kam liquidity wale strike mein trade shuru hote hi bada nuksan ho jaata hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Market_Depth_Levels`, `Liquidity_Slippage_Hadd`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-60 | Med]` Dikkat: "rate ka farak" kaise naapa jaaye, yeh saaf nahi: sirf bid-ask farak se ya poore order ki quantity ko depth mein chala kar. Agar order ki quantity top level ki quantity se zyada ho, toh Slippage depth ke levels mein chal kar naapa jaaye: khareedte waqt ask taraf, bechte waqt bid taraf. Wajah: bade order mein top level ka bhav dhokha deta hai.

### Kharcha_Filter

Naam: Kharcha_Filter. Dekhta hai: Agli OI Deewar tak ka andaza hua munafa banam kul kharcha. Munafa Delta se nikalta hai aur `Time_Stop_Samay` mein Theta ka ghatao ghata diya jaata hai. Kharcha broker ki live charge suchi (brokerage, tax, exchange charge) aur Slippage se aata hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. GREEN kab: Agar andaza hua fayda kul kharchon se kam se kam `Kharcha_Fayda_Guna` ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar andaza hua fayda kul kharchon ke `Kharcha_Fayda_Guna` se kam ho, toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: Option khareedne mein kharche bade hote hain, isliye chhote fayde mein jeb se nuksan hota hai. Bharosa: andaza (kharcha pakka, munafe ka hisaab Delta aur Theta tak seemit). Number: `Kharcha_Fayda_Guna`.

### Strike_Volume_Filter

Naam: Strike_Volume_Filter. Dekhta hai: Chune hue strike ka `Strike_Volume_Lookback_Samay` ka trading volume. Apna average pichle `Volume_Baseline_Din` dinon ke usi samay ke volume se banta hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. GREEN kab: Agar strike ka volume apne average se kam se kam `Strike_Volume_Average_Guna` ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar strike ka volume apne average ke `Strike_Volume_Average_Guna` se kam ho, toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: Index bhaag raha ho par strike mein trading na ho toh woh trap ho sakta hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Strike_Volume_Lookback_Samay`, `Strike_Volume_Average_Guna`, `Volume_Baseline_Din`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-38 | Med]` Dikkat: kisi strike ka pichle dinon ka volume uske aaj ke haal se mel nahi khaata. Har din strike ki ATM se doori aur expiry tak ke bache din badal jaate hain. Agar volume ka average banana ho, toh woh usi Moneyness Khaane aur usi tarah ke bache dinon wale strike ke volume se bane, strike ke naam se nahi. Wajah: galat average se asli volume wala strike bhi RED ho sakta hai.

### Fake_Spike_Filter

Naam: Fake_Spike_Filter. Dekhta hai: Chune hue strike ke saath aas-paas ke `Aas_Paas_Strikes_Ginti` ka premium, ek hi samay ke snapshot mein (`Option_Chain_Snapshot_Samay_Farak`). GREEN kab: Agar tezi ek lehar ki tarah `Aas_Paas_Strikes_Ginti` mein bhi fail rahi ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar sirf ek akela strike bhaage aur baaki so rahe hon, toh "Fake Spike" RED hota hai. NA kab: Agar snapshot ek samay ke na hon.

Wajah: Asli breakout poori Option Chain mein failta hai, akela uchhal nakli hota hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Aas_Paas_Strikes_Ginti`, `Option_Chain_Snapshot_Samay_Farak`.

### Chase_Guard

Naam: Chase_Guard. Dekhta hai: Premium ki badhotri, index ki ATR se doori, agli OI Deewar aur pullback. Breakout woh candle hai jis par `Range_Breakout_Check` pehli baar GREEN hua. Premium ki badhotri us candle ke band hone par strike ke bid-ask ke beech ke bhav se naapi jaati hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. GREEN kab: Agar premium `Chase_Guard_Premium_Badhotri_Hadd` se kam bhaaga ho ya pullback dekar chale, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar premium `Chase_Guard_Premium_Badhotri_Hadd` se upar bhaaga ho aur pullback na aaya ho, toh RED hota hai. OI Deewar paas ho toh wajah "Late Entry" likhi jaati hai, door ho toh "Premium Bhaaga".

Wajah: Bhaage hue mehenge premium ko upar khareedne par pullback mein turant Stop-Loss kat sakta hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Chase_Guard_Premium_Badhotri_Hadd`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-42 | Med]` "Pullback dekar chale" ka paimana `Pullback_Paimana` hai (dekho: Naam-Suchi, Pullback). Wajah: bina paimane ke yeh shart har baar alag padhi jaayegi.

### OI_Deewar_Doori_Gate

Naam: OI_Deewar_Doori_Gate. Dekhta hai: Spot Price se agli badi OI Deewar tak ki jagah (index points mein) aur wahan OI ka badlaav. Bot pehle Stop-Loss nikalta hai, phir uski premium ki doori ko Delta se index points mein badalkar deewar ki doori se milata hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. GREEN kab: Agar deewar Stop-Loss se `OI_Deewar_Stop_Guna` door ho ya wahan Short-Covering ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar deewar `OI_Deewar_Stop_Guna` se paas ho aur OI ghat na raha ho, toh "OI Wall Too Close" RED hota hai.

Wajah: Paas khadi mazboot deewar ke saamne fayda kam aur nuksan zyada hota hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `OI_Deewar_Stop_Guna`.

### Manipulation_Cash_Volume_Check

Naam: Manipulation_Cash_Volume_Check. Dekhta hai: Expiry din (`Manipulation_Check1_Samay`) bade stocks ka cash traded-value aur options bhav va OI (dekho: Tarikh wali baatein). GREEN kab: Agar cash stocks ka volume normal ho aur options ke saath taalmel ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar ek-do stocks mein `Manipulation_Cash_Volume_Guna` volume ho aur options mein ajeeb OI ho, toh "SUSPICIOUS" RED hota hai.

Wajah: Cash stocks ko hila kar options mein trap banane wale pattern se bachna zaroori hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Manipulation_Check1_Samay`, `Manipulation_Cash_Volume_Guna`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-01 | Critical]` Yeh check sirf expiry din aur `Manipulation_Check1_Samay` mein lagu hai; baaki samay yeh NA-Lagu-Nahi hai (dekho: Faisle ka raasta). `[F-02 | Critical]` Nifty50 aur Sensex ke stocks ki suchi tay hone tak un index ke expiry din yeh check NA-Data hoga aur Gate RED karega (Sawal 16). "Ajeeb OI" ka paimana tay nahi hai (`Manipulation_Ajeeb_OI_Paimana`). Wajah: vague shart par Gate ka faisla har baar alag hoga.

### Manipulation_GEX_Mausam_Check

Naam: Manipulation_GEX_Mausam_Check. Dekhta hai: Subah ke Din ka Mausam faisle aur GEX tagging (Positive banam Negative Gamma Day) ka milaan. GREEN kab: Agar subah ka mausam aur GEX dono ek hi baat bolein, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar subah ka mausam Trending bole par GEX Positive Gamma Day bole, toh caution-flag RED hota hai. Group Directional-Bias hai par Asar sirf Confidence hai, isliye yeh Score ki ginti mein nahi aata `[PRASTAAVIT]`.

Wajah: Mausam aur Option Chain positioning mein takraav hone par nakli breakout ka khatra badhta hai. Bharosa: andaza (GEX dealer assumption par bana hai). Shadow Mode mein rehta hai. Number: `Confidence_Badhotri_Points`.

### Manipulation_Settlement_Check

Naam: Manipulation_Settlement_Check. Dekhta hai: Expiry din `Settlement_Manipulation_Check_Samay` baad shant bazaar mein cash stocks ki bhaari khareed-bech (dekho: Tarikh wali baatein). GREEN kab: Agar aakhri ghante mein chaal poore bazaar ke saath normal ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar `Expiry_Settlement_Ausat_Samay` bhav dhakelne ke liye cash stocks mein achanak bhaari saude hon, toh "SUSPICIOUS" RED hota hai. Yeh flag us index mein agli expiry ke trade ko bhi rokta hai.

Wajah: Expiry ke aakhri ghante mein settlement bhav ko dhakelne wale khel se bachna zaroori hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Settlement_Manipulation_Check_Samay`, `Expiry_Settlement_Ausat_Samay`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-01 | Critical]` Yeh check sirf expiry din `Settlement_Manipulation_Check_Samay` ke baad lagu hai; usse pehle yeh NA-Lagu-Nahi hai. "Shant bazaar" aur "bhaari saude" ka paimana tay nahi (`Settlement_Bhaari_Saude_Paimana`). `[F-32 | Med]` SUSPICIOUS flag us index mein `SUSPICIOUS_Flag_Avadhi` tak rehta hai. Wajah: flag ka ant tay na ho toh agle din ka trade bhi bina wajah ruk sakta hai.

### Lambi_Wick_Check

Naam: Lambi_Wick_Check. Dekhta hai: Breakout candle ke band hote hi uske upar ya neeche bani wick (rejection). Yeh check sirf us band candle par chalta hai, adhoori par nahi `[PRASTAAVIT]`. GREEN kab: Agar candle ki wick `Lambi_Wick_Hadd` se chhoti ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar candle mein wick `Lambi_Wick_Hadd` se lambi ho, toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: Lambi wick dikhati hai ki breakout level par bhaari ulta dabav maujood hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Lambi_Wick_Hadd`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-42 | Med]` Breakout Candle `Breakout_Candle_Timeframe` ki hoti hai. Yahi candle Range_Breakout_Check, Chase_Guard aur Trap_Detection_Check mein bhi maani jaati hai. Wajah: chaaron check ek hi candle dekhein, warna aapas mein takraayenge.

### Tick_Heartbeat_Check

Naam: Tick_Heartbeat_Check. Dekhta hai: Spot Price, Futures aur Option Chain ke tick data ka Heartbeat Timestamp aur backup stream. Bot ghadi ke farak ko bhi dekhta hai (`Ghadi_Skew_Hadd`). Aam taur par kam chalne wale strike ka data apni aam gap se aanka jaata hai, feed ke gap se nahi `[PRASTAAVIT]`. GREEN kab: Agar index, Futures, ATM strikes aur chune hue strike ka data `Data_Purana_Hadd` ke andar ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar inmein se koi bhi purana ya atka ho, ya connection judne ke baad abhi `Reconnect_Taza_Tick_Ginti` taaza tick na aaye hon, toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: Broker ka data atakne par purane bhav par calculation karne se galat trade lag sakta hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Data_Purana_Hadd`, `Ghadi_Skew_Hadd`, `Reconnect_Taza_Tick_Ginti`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-07 | High]` Data ki umar do tarah se naapi jaaye: exchange ki chhap se, aur bot ke apne data paane ke samay se. Agar dono mein se koi bhi `Data_Purana_Hadd` se purana ho, toh RED. Ghadi ki jaanch `Ghadi_Skew_Sakht_Hadd` se ho (dekho: Bot ka ek poora din). Wajah: ghadi ki galti data ki taazgi ka naap bigaad deti hai.

### Broker_Exchange_Gadbad_Check

Naam: Broker_Exchange_Gadbad_Check. Dekhta hai: Broker connection, exchange trading halt, index aur option ka circuit-limit, Static IP ya Algo Name ka reject aur lagatar reject hote orders. GREEN kab: Agar connection theek ho, exchange normal ho aur orders reject na ho rahe hon, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar connection toote, exchange halt ho, bhav `Circuit_Paas_Hadd` ke andar ho, `Order_Reject_Ginti` pooree ho ya Static IP ya Algo Name reject aaye, toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: System ya exchange ki gadbad mein naya order lagana khatarnak hota hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Circuit_Paas_Hadd`, `Order_Reject_Ginti`.

### Call_Put_Takraav_Gate

Naam: Call_Put_Takraav_Gate. Dekhta hai: Ek hi index mein Call-Put dono pass hona ya doosre index mein ulta trade khula hona. Foreign Position ko yeh ginta nahi. GREEN kab: Agar sirf ek hi disha pass ho aur doosre index mein ulta trade na ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar Call-Put dono ek saath pass hon ya doosre index mein ulta trade ho, toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: Ek hi waqt par dono dishaon mein trade lena apne hi trade se ladna hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: tay nahi.

### Premium_Tezi_Check

Naam: Premium_Tezi_Check. Dekhta hai: Option premium ki raftaar, usi strike ki pichli 5 minute ki apni ausat raftaar se. Gamma Explosion isi raftaar ka dugna hissa hai, alag shart nahi `[PRASTAAVIT]`. GREEN kab: Agar premium `Premium_Tezi_Samay_Aur_Percent` ki raftaar se badhe, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar premium mein tay raftaar ki tezi na ho, toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: Option khareedne mein tez raftaar hi Theta Decay ko hara sakti hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Premium_Tezi_Samay_Aur_Percent`.

### Volume_Uchhal_Check

Naam: Volume_Uchhal_Check. Dekhta hai: Option va Futures volume ka uchhal, VWAP aur Futures premium/discount (dekho: Tayyar Code (Repo)). VWAP din ke shuru se gina jaata hai, ghoomti khidki se nahi. Futures woh contract liya jaata hai jiska volume zyada ho `[PRASTAAVIT]`. GREEN kab: Agar volume `Volume_Uchhal_Guna` uchhle, bhav VWAP se upar ho aur Futures discount mein na phisle, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar volume na uchhle, bhav VWAP se neeche ho ya Futures discount mein phisle, toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: Spot index mein volume nahi hota, isliye asli khareedari Futures volume aur VWAP se dikhti hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Volume_Uchhal_Guna`, `Volume_Baseline_Din`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-39 | Med]` Dikkat: GREEN aur RED ki shart sirf Call ke hisaab se likhi hai. Agar trade Put ka ho, toh shartein ulti padhi jaayein: bhav VWAP se neeche ho aur Futures premium mein na uchhle. Yahi ulta niyam Iceberg_Order_Check par bhi lage. "Futures discount mein phisalna" ka paimana `Futures_Discount_Hadd` hai. Wajah: Put ke liye PRD wala AI ulta niyam na bana de.

### Rate_Update_Gati_Check

Naam: Rate_Update_Gati_Check. Dekhta hai: Broker feed mein ek second mein bhav update hone ki ginti banam normal gati. GREEN kab: Agar rate update ki gati feed ki normal gati se `Rate_Update_Gati_Guna` badh jaaye, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar rate update ki gati normal hi rahe, toh RED hota hai. NA kab: Agar feed sirf thodi der ka sanket deta ho aur ginti bharose ki na ho.

Wajah: Bade khiladiyon wale breakout mein rate badalne ki raftaar kai guna badh jaati hai. Bharosa: andaza (feed ki gati broker par bhi tiki hai). Shadow Mode mein rehta hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Number: `Rate_Update_Gati_Guna`.

### Iceberg_Order_Check

Naam: Iceberg_Order_Check. Dekhta hai: Broker ki Market Depth mein kisi strike par lagatar aane wale bade orders aur bhav tikav. GREEN kab: Agar lagatar bade orders aa rahe hon par bhav neeche na gir raha ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar bade orders aane ke baad bhi bhav neeche gire, toh RED hota hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. NA kab: Agar top levels ka data na mile.

Wajah: Yeh sanket deta hai ki koi bada khiladi chhote hisson mein chhupa kar khareed raha hai. Bharosa: andaza (probabilistic volume-pattern proxy, asli poori order-book nahi; chhupa hissa top levels mein nahi dikhta). Shadow Mode mein rehta hai. Number: `Market_Depth_Levels`.

### Trap_Detection_Check

Naam: Trap_Detection_Check. Dekhta hai: Support ya resistance tootne ke baad breakout ka tikav ya wapas range mein ghusna. Tikav ka samay breakout ki pehli tick se ginta hai; bade timeframe ki disha is dauran pichli band candle ki maani jaati hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. GREEN kab: Agar mazboot breakout `Mazboot_Breakout_Tikav_Samay` tike, kamzor `Kamzor_Breakout_Tikav_Samay` tike, ya trap par ulta trade ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar breakout tay samay tak na tike ya ulte trade ke liye trap na bane, toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: Retail Stop-Loss udane wale trap se bachna aur asli tez move ko bhi na chhodna. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Mazboot_Breakout_Tikav_Samay`, `Kamzor_Breakout_Tikav_Samay`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-42 | Med]` Mazboot Breakout aur Kamzor Breakout ka farak `Mazboot_Breakout_Paribhasha` se hai. `[F-57 | Low]` Band candle ke intezaar mein tikav ka samay aksar apne aap poora ho jaata hai. Isliye yeh check Range_Breakout_Check ki hi Data Family mein hai. Wajah: ek hi baat do baar points na de.

### Range_Breakout_Check

Naam: Range_Breakout_Check. Dekhta hai: Pichle `Chart_Range_Breakout_Samay` ki range (aur subah pichle din va opening range) ka breakout, poori band candle par. GREEN kab: Agar band candle tay range ko Call ke liye upar ya Put ke liye neeche tode, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar bhav abhi range ke andar hi atka ho, toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: Sirf premium nahi balki index ka chart bhi breakout ka samarthan karna chahiye. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Chart_Range_Breakout_Samay`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-42 | Med]` Breakout `Breakout_Candle_Timeframe` ki band candle par dekha jaata hai, aur "opening range" `Opening_Range_Samay` ki hai. `[F-55 | Low]` `Chart_Range_Breakout_Samay` ek range hai; bot ek hi kimat chune (Sawal 133). Wajah: do kimat par do alag breakout banenge.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Hypothesis H-14: Spot index mein asli saude nahi hote, isliye breakout Futures ke bhav par jaldi aur saaf dikh sakta hai. Test: Backtest mein Spot wale aur Futures wale breakout ki Net Expectancy aur deri milao. Wajah: jaldi aur saaf signal Slippage aur Chase ka nuksan ghatata hai.

### Timeframe_Match_Check

Naam: Timeframe_Match_Check. Dekhta hai: Bade timeframe (`Chart_Bada_Timeframe`) ka trend aur chhote timeframe (`Chart_Chhota_Timeframe`) ki entry disha, dono Disha ki ek hi paribhasha se. GREEN kab: Agar `Chart_Bada_Timeframe` ka trend aur `Chart_Chhota_Timeframe` ki entry ek hi disha mein hon, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar dono timeframes ki disha alag ho, toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: Bade trend ki disha mein chhote timeframe par entry lene se safalta badhti hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Chart_Bada_Timeframe`, `Chart_Chhota_Timeframe`, `Disha_Paribhasha`.

### EMA_Slope_Check

Naam: EMA_Slope_Check. Dekhta hai: Chart par `EMA_Periods` lines ka jhukaav (slope) (dekho: Tayyar Code (Repo)). GREEN kab: Agar `EMA_Periods` ka slope Call ke liye upar ya Put ke liye neeche sharp ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar `EMA_Periods` flat hon ya ulti disha mein hon, toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: Sharp EMA slope batata hai ki trend mein tez raftaar hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `EMA_Periods`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-42 | Med]` "Sharp" slope ka paimana `EMA_Slope_Hadd` hai, jo abhi Tay nahi. Agar yeh Backtest se tay na ho, toh yeh check score mein na gine aur Shadow Mode mein chale. Wajah: bina paimane ka GREEN bharose ka nahi.

### RSI_Momentum_Check

Naam: RSI_Momentum_Check. Dekhta hai: Chart par RSI meter ka level (dekho: Tayyar Code (Repo)). GREEN kab: Agar Call mein RSI `RSI_Bullish_Hadd` upar ya Put mein `RSI_Bearish_Hadd` neeche ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar RSI tezi ya mandi ke daayron ke bahar beech mein ho, toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: RSI pakka karta hai ki bhav ki chaal mein asli momentum maujood hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `RSI_Bullish_Hadd`, `RSI_Bearish_Hadd`.

### Ulta_Palat_Reversal_Chart_Check

Naam: Ulta_Palat_Reversal_Chart_Check. Dekhta hai: Ulta-Palat mausam mein support/resistance trap, RSI divergence aur VWAP ki taraf wapsi. Divergence ke pivot tabhi gine jaate hain jab unke baad ki poori band candles aa chuki hon `[PRASTAAVIT]`. GREEN kab: Agar Ulta-Palat din mein trap, RSI divergence aur VWAP ki taraf wapsi dikhe, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar Ulta-Palat mausam mein yeh reversal sanket na milein, toh RED hota hai. NA kab: Agar mausam Ulta-Palat nahi hai.

Wajah: Palat-te bazaar mein normal trend indicators ke bajay reversal sanket sahi entry dete hain. Bharosa: andaza (divergence ka hisaab pivot ki avadhi par tika hai). Number: `Ulta_Palat_Pehchaan_Paimana`.

### Volatility_Squeeze_Check

Naam: Volatility_Squeeze_Check. Dekhta hai: Bollinger Bands aur Keltner Channel ka sikudna (Squeeze) aur phir khulna (dekho: Tayyar Code (Repo)). GREEN kab: Agar bands `Squeeze_Bands_Parameters` ke hisaab se sikudne ke baad trade ki disha mein phat rahe hon, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar squeeze na mile ya bands ulti disha mein phatein, toh RED hota hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`.

Wajah: Sikude hue bands dikhate hain ki bazaar dabi hui spring ban gaya hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Shadow Mode mein rehta hai. Number: `Squeeze_Bands_Parameters`.

### Order_Block_BOS_CHoCH_FVG_Check

Naam: Order_Block_BOS_CHoCH_FVG_Check. Dekhta hai: Candle chart par Order Block, BOS, CHoCH aur Fair Value Gap (FVG) (dekho: Tayyar Code (Repo)). Sirf poori band candles par chalta hai; swing tabhi gina jaata hai jab uske baad ki candles aa chuki hon `[PRASTAAVIT]`. GREEN kab: Agar entry disha mein Order Block, BOS ya CHoCH aur FVG ka samarthan mile, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar entry ki ulti disha mein BOS ya CHoCH bane, toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: Yeh chart structure aur candle gaps se entry ke liye extra confirmation deta hai. Bharosa: andaza (probabilistic volume-pattern proxy, asli institutional order-book nahi). Shadow Mode mein rehta hai. Number: tay nahi.

### Max_Pain_Shift_Check

Naam: Max_Pain_Shift_Check. Dekhta hai: Sirf expiry din Spot Price ki Max Pain se doori aur Max Pain ka shift hona (dekho: Tayyar Code (Repo)). GREEN kab: Agar bhav Max Pain ki taraf khinche ya Max Pain trade disha mein shift ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar bhav Max Pain se door jaaye aur shift ulti disha mein ho, toh RED hota hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. NA kab: Agar aaj expiry din nahi ya `Max_Pain_Paas_Hadd` ka samay nahi aaya.

Wajah: Expiry par Max Pain ka shift option writers ki chhupi positioning dikhata hai. Bharosa: andaza (yeh sirf ek jhukaav hai, pakka niyam nahi). Shadow Mode mein rehta hai. Number: `Max_Pain_Paas_Hadd`.

### GEX_Positioning_Check

Naam: GEX_Positioning_Check. Dekhta hai: Option Chain se dealers ke long aur short gamma levels (dekho: Tayyar Code (Repo)). Participant-Wise Data har shaam aata hai aur participant ke hisaab se hota hai, strike ke hisaab se nahi, isliye woh GEX ka srot nahi hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. GREEN kab: Agar bhav short gamma zone mein ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar bhav long gamma zone mein phansa ho jahan chaal dheemi hoti hai, toh RED hota hai. NA kab: Agar strike-wise dealer data ka srot na ho.

Wajah: Short gamma zone mein bhav tezi se ek disha mein bhaagta hai. Bharosa: andaza (standard GEX US assumption par bana hai). Shadow Mode mein rehta hai. Number: tay nahi.

### Gamma_Flip_Check

Naam: Gamma_Flip_Check. Dekhta hai: Spot Price ki Gamma Flip level se doori aur disha (dekho: Tayyar Code (Repo)). GREEN kab: Agar Spot Price Gamma Flip se saaf door breakout wale hisse mein ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar bhav Gamma Flip ke `Gamma_Flip_Paas_Hadd` mein ho aur range wale hisse mein ho, toh RED hota hai. Agar bhav `Gamma_Flip_Paas_Hadd` mein ho par breakout wale hisse mein ho, toh sirf confidence ghat-ta hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Baaki sab haal mein 0 point.

Wajah: Gamma Flip ke bilkul paas bazaar ka mood badalta rehta hai. Bharosa: andaza. Shadow Mode mein rehta hai. Number: `Gamma_Flip_Paas_Hadd`, `Confidence_Badhotri_Points`.

### IV_Skew_Check

Naam: IV_Skew_Check. Dekhta hai: Call strikes aur Put strikes ke IV ke badhne ki raftaar ka farak, bid-ask ke beech ke bhav se. GREEN kab: Agar Call ke liye Calls IV ya Put ke liye Puts IV tezi se badhe, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar ulti side ka IV zyada tezi se badh raha ho, toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: Jis taraf ke options ki maang zyada hoti hai, us taraf ka IV tez badhta hai. Bharosa: andaza (kam trading wale strike mein IV hilta hai). Shadow Mode mein rehta hai. Number: tay nahi.

### OI_Deewar_Short_Covering_Check

Naam: OI_Deewar_Short_Covering_Check. Dekhta hai: Major strikes par naya OI banna aur option writers ki Short-Covering (dekho: Tayyar Code (Repo)). OI Option Chain snapshot ki raftaar se badalta hai, yaani yeh minuton ka sanket hai, seconds ka nahi `[PRASTAAVIT]`. GREEN kab: Agar peeche OI support deewar bane aur aage ki deewar par Short-Covering ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar trade ki ulti disha mein naya OI bhaari matra mein ban raha ho, toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: Option writers jab dar kar bhagte hain toh sabse tez move aata hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Option_Chain_Snapshot_Samay_Farak`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-44 | Med]` Dikkat: OI exchange se der-der mein badalta hai, isliye yeh sabse dheema sanket hai, par iska vazan sabse bade mein se hai. "Bhaari matra mein naya OI" ka paimana bhi tay nahi (`OI_Bhaari_Badlaav_Paimana`). Hypothesis H-11: Ablation mein dekho ki is check ka vazan sach mein utna faayda deta hai ya nahi. Wajah: purane data par bada vazan galat breakout ko pass kar sakta hai.

### Pre_Breakout_Alert_Check

Naam: Pre_Breakout_Alert_Check. Dekhta hai: Shant index aur bina news ke kam se kam do paas ke strikes ka premium aur OI ek saath achanak badhna `[PRASTAAVIT]`. GREEN kab: Agar shant index mein bina khabar aur `IVP_Mehenga_Hadd` ke andar premium va OI uchhle, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar uchhal sirf ek akele strike mein ho ya IVP `IVP_Mehenga_Hadd` se upar ho, toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: Chart breakout se pehle hi bade khiladiyon ki chupke se khareedari ki aahat mil jaati hai. Bharosa: andaza. Shadow Mode mein rehta hai. Number: `IVP_Mehenga_Hadd`.

### PCR_Trend_Check

Naam: PCR_Trend_Check. Dekhta hai: Option Chain ka Put-Call Ratio (PCR) aur uske badalne ki disha (dekho: Tayyar Code (Repo)). PCR badhna Call ke haq mein aur ghatna Put ke haq mein maana jaata hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. GREEN kab: Agar PCR ka badlaav trade ki disha ke haq mein ho, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar PCR trade ki ulti disha mein ja raha ho, toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: PCR ke badalne ki disha batati hai ki option writers kis taraf jhuk rahe hain. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Option_Chain_Snapshot_Samay_Farak`.

### Teeno_Index_Disha_Check

Naam: Teeno_Index_Disha_Check. Dekhta hai: BankNifty, Nifty50 aur Sensex teeno ki Disha. GREEN kab: Agar teeno indices ek hi Disha mein hon, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar teeno indices alag Disha mein hon, toh RED hota hai aur confidence kam karta hai. Teeno ek saath chalte hain, isliye ek disha ke do khule trade ek hi bade daon ki tarah ginte jaate hain `[PRASTAAVIT]`.

Wajah: Teeno indices ek saath ek disha mein hon tabhi chaal pakki maani jaati hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Index_Ginti`, `Disha_Paribhasha`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-40 | Med]` Dikkat: "teeno alag Disha" ka matlab saaf nahi, jaise jab do ek Disha mein hon aur ek FLAT. Agar teeno trade ki Disha mein hon toh GREEN, aur koi ek bhi ulti Disha mein ho toh RED. Baaki haal mein 0 point aur confidence par asar nahi. Wajah: beech ki haalat ka faisla PRD wala AI na kare. "Ek bada daon" ka matlab Expiry ke din ke niyam ke `[F-31]` wale niyam jaisa hai.

### Index_Breadth_Check

Naam: Index_Breadth_Check. Dekhta hai: BankNifty ke `BankNifty_Kul_Banks_Ginti` (aur Nifty50 va Sensex ke top stocks) ka Advance/Decline, vazan ke saath. GREEN kab: Agar vazan ke hisaab se zyada-tar stocks breakout ki disha mein hon, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar vazan ke hisaab se `Khokhla_Breadth_Hadd` ya zyada hissa ulti disha mein ho, toh RED hokar Gate se trade block karta hai. Ruka hua (halt) stock hata kar baaki ke vazan naye sire se baante jaate hain `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Gate RED hone par yeh Sentiment ke GREEN mein nahi ginta.

Wajah: Sirf ek-do stocks ke dam par bana breakout andar se khokhla operator-trap hota hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `BankNifty_Kul_Banks_Ginti`, `Khokhla_Breadth_Hadd`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-02 | Critical]` `[TAKRAAV #32]` Dikkat: yeh check Gate bhi hai, aur Nifty50 va Sensex ke stocks ki suchi Tay nahi hai. Data na hone se yeh NA-Data hoga aur Gate RED karega, yaani Nifty50 aur Sensex mein kabhi trade nahi hoga. Agar malik suchi tay na kare, toh suchi index ke official vazan wale page se har mahine bane, aur sabse bade vazan ke `Breadth_Top_Stocks_Ginti` stocks liye jaayein. Jab tak suchi pakki na ho, Nifty50 aur Sensex sirf Paper Mode mein chalein. Wajah: Gate ka data hi na ho toh Gate poore index ko band kar deta hai.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-58 | Low]` BankNifty ke banks ki ginti exchange badal sakta hai. Agar index ki official suchi mein ginti badle, toh check usi suchi se chale, aur `BankNifty_Kul_Banks_Ginti` ka farak malik ko dikhaya jaaye (dekho: Tarikh wali baatein). Wajah: purani suchi par Breadth galat niklega.

### Heavyweight_Stocks_Check

Naam: Heavyweight_Stocks_Check. Dekhta hai: Index ke Heavyweight Stocks (HDFC Bank, ICICI Bank, Reliance, Infosys aadi) ki Disha. GREEN kab: Agar Heavyweight Stocks trade ki disha mein chal rahe hon, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar Heavyweight Stocks trade ki ulti disha mein hon, toh RED hota hai. Ruka hua stock hata diya jaata hai; agar adhe se zyada heavyweight ruke hon toh check NA hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`.

Wajah: Bade vazan wale stocks ke bina index ka breakout tik nahi paata. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `Disha_Paribhasha`.

### Macro_Divergence_Check

Naam: Macro_Divergence_Check. Dekhta hai: USD/INR, crude oil aur Bharat ke `Bond_Yield_Avadhi` bond yield ka live bhav (dekho: Tayyar Code (Repo)). Dollar-rupaye ka upar jaana, tel ka upar jaana aur yield ka upar jaana Call ke khilaaf aur Put ke haq mein maana jaata hai; neeche jaana ulta `[PRASTAAVIT]`. GREEN kab: Agar yeh macro sanket shant hon ya trade ki disha ke anukool hon, toh GREEN hota hai. RED kab: Agar USD/INR ya bond yield trade ke ulta bhaagein, toh confidence ghat-ta hai aur tez divergence par Gate block karta hai. NA kab: Agar live bhav ka srot na ho.

Wajah: Dollar aur bond yield ka uchhalna banks ke girne se kuch second pehle dikhta hai. Bharosa: andaza (live srot pakka nahi). Shadow Mode mein rehta hai jab tak broker ka seedha feed na mile. Number: `Bond_Yield_Avadhi`.

### VIX_Gati_Term_Structure_Check

Naam: VIX_Gati_Term_Structure_Check. Dekhta hai: India VIX ka level, uske badhne ki speed aur Term-Structure. GREEN kab: Put ke liye VIX `VIX_Medium_Level` se tezi se badhe aur index gire; Call ke liye VIX na badhe ya gire aur index chadhe `[PRASTAAVIT]`. RED kab: Call ke liye VIX tezi se badh raha ho, ya Put ke liye VIX ooncha hokar gir raha ho (jo Theta Decay ke haq mein hai), toh RED hota hai.

Wajah: Option buyer ko tezi se badhta hua VIX hi directional move deta hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: `VIX_Medium_Level`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-42 | Med]` "Tezi se badhe" aur Term-Structure ki shart ka paimana tay nahi (`VIX_Tezi_Paimana`). Wajah: pehle version ke check ki shart number ke bina test nahi ho sakti.

### FII_DII_Macro_Context_Check

Naam: FII_DII_Macro_Context_Check. Dekhta hai: Pichli shaam ka Participant-Wise Data (FII, DII, Pro, Client net long/short) (dekho: Tayyar Code (Repo)). GREEN kab: Agar institutions ki net positioning trade disha se mile, toh confidence-threshold halka rehta hai. RED kab: Agar institutions ki net positioning ulti ho, toh agle din ka confidence-threshold sakht ho jaata hai.

Wajah: Yeh shaam ko aata hai isliye live score mein nahi ginta, par agle din ka context deta hai. Bharosa: pakka data. Number: tay nahi.

## 8. Score, Gate aur Confidence ka hisaab

Bot har index ke liye Call side aur Put side ka alag scorecard banata hai. Scorecard mein checks ko `Scorecard_Groups_Ginti` groups mein baanta jaata hai: Order-Flow, Technical, Directional-Bias aur Sentiment. Har check ka apna vazan hota hai jo Check Suchi mein likha hai. Shadow Mode ke checks scorecard mein nahi ginte `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: bahut saare chhote GREEN milkar bhi ek bade pakke RED ko na daba sakein.

Khareedne ke liye scorecard ki teeno shartein ek saath poori honi chahiye. Agar har group mein `Prati_Group_Minimum_Green` ho, kul `Kul_Minimum_Green_Checks` hon, aur score `Minimum_Weighted_Score` se upar ho, tabhi score pass hota hai. Wajah: har pehlu (raftaar, chart, Option Chain aur mood) ki sahmati ek saath honi chahiye.

Ek akela group us waqt ke zaroori score ke `Ek_Group_Maximum_Score_Hissa` se zyada points nahi de sakta `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Yahan zaroori score matlab confidence badhotri ke baad wala score hai, kul sambhav score nahi. Wajah: ek hi data (jaise volume) se bane checks akele trade pass na kar dein. Trending din par saare pehle-version Score checks ka kul vazan `Check_Vazan_Kul_Jod` hai.

Kuch checks score ka hissa nahi hote balki seedhe Gate hote hain (dekho: Check Suchi). Agar koi bhi ek Gate check RED ho, toh bot trade turant skip kar deta hai. Wajah: suraksha, liquidity, manipulation ya khokhle Breadth par koi samjhauta nahi ho sakta.

Kuch sthitiyan bot ka confidence kam karti hain, aur har ek alag kaaran ginti hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Kaaran yeh hain: `IVP_Medium_Range`, Teeno_Index_Disha_Check ka farak, ulta FII_DII_Macro_Context_Check aur `Lagatar_Ulte_Auto_Entries_Ginti`. Gamma_Flip_Check, Manipulation_GEX_Mausam_Check aur Macro_Divergence_Check bhi kaaran hain, par woh Shadow Mode se bahar aane par hi ginte jaayenge. Har kaaran par `Minimum_Weighted_Score` `Confidence_Badhotri_Points` se upar jaata hai `[TAKRAAV #20]`. Wajah: kamzor sthiti mein bot sirf bahut mazboot signal par hi trade le.

Agar zaroori score `Maximum_Score_Threshold_Cap` se upar chala jaaye, toh bot us index mein aaj trade nahi leta `[TAKRAAV #10]`. Wajah: jab bahut saare khatre ek saath hon toh trade lena galti hogi. Cap badhotri ke kul jod se chhota rakha gaya hai, taaki yeh shart sach mein lag sake `[PRASTAAVIT]`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-43 | Med]` Dikkat: kai checks ek hi data se bante hain, isliye "bahut saare GREEN" asal mein kam alag saboot hain. EMA_Slope_Check, Timeframe_Match_Check, Teeno_Index_Disha_Check aur Heavyweight_Stocks_Check sab Disha par tike hain (dekho: Check Suchi ki Data Family table). Group cap sirf Group dekhta hai, data ka dohraav nahi. Agar Ablation dikhaye ki ek Data Family ke checks ek saath chalte hain, toh us family ke kul points `Data_Family_Max_Score_Hissa` se zyada na ginein. Wajah: ek hi baat ke kai GREEN nakli bharosa dete hain.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Hypothesis H-11: har check ka asli faayda Ablation se naapa jaaye. Jo check kharche ke baad Net Expectancy na badhaye, uska vazan malik ki manzoori se ghatana ya use Shadow Mode mein bhejna socha jaaye. Test: `03_Strategy_Edge_Plan.md` ka Ablation plan. Wajah: vazan andaze se hain, data se nahi.

## 9. Risk aur Lot ginti

Ek Lot ki quantity exchange tay karta hai (dekho: Tarikh wali baatein). Ek Lot ka kharcha chune hue premium ko `Ek_Lot_Quantity` se guna karke nikalta hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: Target_Premium_Range ek unit ka bhav hai, Lot ka kul kharcha nahi.

Har trade se pehle bot broker account mein asli free paisa aur margin dekhta hai. Agar chune hue strike ka premium `Target_Premium_Range` mein ho aur free paisa 1 Lot ke kharche aur `Balance_Extra_Buffer` se zyada ho, toh bot jitne poore Lot ka paisa ho utne Lot leta hai (`Entry_Lot_Ginti`). Saare Lot ek hi order aur ek hi trade mein jaate hain, doosra Lot alag trade nahi banta `[TAKRAAV #15]` `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: user ne kaha ki bot balance ke hisaab se khareede.

Bot subah 1 Lot ka kharcha aur `Balance_Extra_Buffer` jodkar batata hai ki aaj kitna paisa chahiye (`Fund_Request_Udaharan`). Agar malik zyada paisa daale, toh Lot asli free paisa se tay hote hain, Fund Request se nahi `[TAKRAAV #13]` `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: Lot ginti hamesha broker ke asli free paisa par tiki hai. Account mein abhi lagbhag `Account_Balance_Udaharan` rehta hai.

Lot balance ki upari seema se kabhi zyada nahi hote. Agar signal ka score zaroori score se `Mazboot_Signal_Score_Margin` zyada ho, toh bot balance ki seema tak Lot leta hai. Warna woh `Mazboot_Signal_Extra_Lot` ke hisaab se ek Lot kam leta hai, par `Minimum_Trade_Lot` se neeche nahi `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: bharose ke hisaab se risk sadha rahe aur `Entry_Lot_Ginti` se takraav na ho.

Agar Din ka Mausam Ulta-Palat ho ya account peak se `Account_Peak_Drawdown_Hadd` neeche ho, toh Lot `Ulta_Palat_Aur_Drawdown_Lot_Cut` ho jaate hain `[TAKRAAV #17]`. Aadhe Lot neeche ki taraf round hote hain, aur 1 Lot par yeh cut nahi lagta kyunki 1 Lot aadha nahi hota `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Dono kaaran ek saath hon toh bhi Lot sirf ek baar aadhe hote hain. Account sudharne par Lot `Lot_Wapasi_Paimana` ke hisaab se ek-ek karke wapas aate hain. Wajah: kharab daur ya palat-te bazaar mein bada nuksan na ho.

Peak aur drawdown Deposit-Adjusted Equity se naape jaate hain, jama-nikasi se nahi `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: roz paisa judne se girawat ka hisaab na bigde. Agar account mein `Minimum_Trade_Lot` ke poore paise na hon, toh bot strike chhod deta hai. Wajah: bot apni Risk Limit se zyada paisa kabhi nahi lagata.

Agar khule trade mein bhav ulta chale, toh bot us trade mein aur Lot nahi jodta `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: average down se ek hi trade ka nuksan badhta hai. Ek trade ka nuksan sirf `Premium_Max_Stop_Loss_Percent` se bandha hai, alag rupaye ki hadd nahi `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: percent wali per-trade hadd user ne hata di thi.

Agar band hue trades ka kul nuksan `Daily_Loss_Risk_Limit` paar kare, toh bot naye entry band karke khule trades nikaalta hai `[TAKRAAV #17]`. Yeh hadd trade band hone ke baad ginti jaati hai, trade ke beech mein nahi `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Poora balance 1 Lot mein lagne se ek Stop-Loss bhi is hadd se bada ho sakta hai; yeh jaanboojh kar maana gaya nuksan hai. Wajah: poore balance se 1 Lot lena user ka tay kiya niyam hai. Agar hafte ka nuksan `Weekly_Loss_Risk_Limit` ya mahine ka nuksan `Monthly_Loss_Risk_Limit` paar kare, toh bot us avadhi mein naye entry nahi leta `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Mahine ki hadd par bot tabhi chalu hota hai jab malik report dekh kar haath se Start Control dabaye. Wajah: kharab daur mein lagatar nuksan rokna zaroori hai.

Kamai bachane ke liye bot mein Daily Profit Lock hota hai. Agar subah ke trades se `Daily_Profit_Lock_Trigger` kamai ho jaaye, toh bot `Daily_Profit_Lock_Hissa` kamai lock kar leta hai. Uske baad bot sirf wahi trade leta hai jiska poora Stop-Loss nuksan bache hue (lock se upar ke) munafe ke andar aaye `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Agar aisa trade na ban sake, toh us din naye entry band rehte hain. Agar kamai ghat kar lock level ko chhoo le, toh bot us din trading band kar deta hai. Wajah: subah ka bada munafa dopahar ke whipsaw mein wapas bazar ko na jaaye.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-03 | Critical]` `[TAKRAAV #28]` Dikkat: poore balance se Lot lene par ek hi Stop-Loss ka nuksan `Daily_Loss_Risk_Limit`, `Weekly_Loss_Risk_Limit` aur `Monthly_Loss_Risk_Limit` teeno se bada ho sakta hai. Tab pehle hi nuksan ke baad bot poore mahine ruk jaata hai, jab tak malik haath se Start na de. Ganit yeh bhi dikhata hai ki har trade mein poora balance lagane par, ausat fayda positive hone par bhi, lambe samay mein balance ghat sakta hai (dekho: `03_Strategy_Edge_Plan.md`, Risk of Ruin). Malik ka niyam waisa hi hai; neeche ke vikalp sirf Prastaav hain. Wajah: risk ki seemayein aur Lot ka niyam ek doosre se mel nahi khaate.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Vikalp A (Hissa): ek trade mein Deposit-Adjusted Equity ka zyada se zyada `Fractional_Stake_Hissa` premium mein lage; 1 Lot usse mehenga ho toh trade skip. Vikalp B (Stop-Unit seema): Risk Limit percent ke bajay Stop-Unit mein gine jaayein: `Stop_Unit_Din_Seema`, `Stop_Unit_Hafta_Seema` aur `Stop_Unit_Mahina_Seema`. Vikalp C (Rupaye ki hadd): malik ek trade ka zyada se zyada Worst-Case Nuksan rupaye mein tay kare (`Prati_Trade_Max_Nuksan_Rupaye`); usse bada trade skip. Wajah: risk ki seema aur Lot ka niyam ek hi ikai mein hon.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Hypothesis H-09 aur H-10: Monte Carlo aur Backtest mein malik ka niyam aur teeno vikalp milaye jaayein: Risk of Ruin, sabse badi girawat aur lambe samay ki badhotri. Agar malik ke niyam ki Risk of Ruin vikalp se bahut zyada nikle, toh malik se dobara faisla liya jaaye (Sawal 118). Wajah: faisla ganit aur data se ho, andaze se nahi.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-15 | High]` Dikkat: "ek trade ka nuksan `Premium_Max_Stop_Loss_Percent` se bandha hai" gap ya tez girawat mein sach nahi. Bhav Stop-Loss ko kood sakta hai, Broker Stop aur door hota hai, aur MPP Order ka bhav aur neeche ja sakta hai. Agar risk ka koi bhi hisaab ho (Risk Limit, Lot ginti, Daily Profit Lock), toh ek trade ka nuksan Worst-Case Nuksan se gina jaaye. Wajah: kaagaz par chhota dikhta nuksan asal mein bada ho sakta hai. Asar: kam Lot ya kam trade, par seema sach mein bachti hai.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-14 | High]` Dikkat: din ka nuksan sirf band trade se gina jaata hai, isliye ek khula trade gehre nuksan mein ho tab bhi doosra trade khul sakta hai. Agar naya entry lena ho, toh band trades ka nuksan aur har Khule Trade ka Worst-Case Nuksan milakar `Daily_Loss_Risk_Limit` ke andar ho. Wajah: do trade ek saath girein toh din ki seema ek hi jhatke mein toot sakti hai. Asar: doosra trade kam baar khulega.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-50 | Low]` Daily Profit Lock "subah ke trades" par likha hai. Agar kamai dopahar mein `Daily_Profit_Lock_Trigger` tak pahunche, toh bhi lock lage ya nahi, yeh malik se pushti ho (Sawal 132). Poore balance wale Lot mein lock ke baad bacha munafa ek Stop-Loss ke liye kam padta hai, isliye lock lagte hi din lagbhag band ho jaata hai. Wajah: niyam ka asli asar malik ko pata ho.

## 10. Entry

Jab scorecard ki teeno shartein aur saare Gates pass hon, toh bot khud BUY order lagata hai `[TAKRAAV #1]`. Wajah: tez chaal pakadne ke liye turant automatic entry zaroori hai. Bot sirf Start Control dabane ke baad entry leta hai `[TAKRAAV #1]`. Start se pehle bot sirf dekhta hai aur Fund Request bhejta hai `[TAKRAAV #16]`. Wajah: Start malik ki us din ki poori ijaazat hai.

Order se pehle bot Intent Log likhta hai aur order par Order Tag lagata hai (dekho: Pehredaar aur Suraksha). Bot market order nahi lagata, balki ask bhav ke upar `Entry_Order_Slippage_Buffer` tak Limit Order lagata hai (dekho: Tayyar Code (Repo)). Bhav Tick Size par round hota hai aur Circuit-limit ke andar rehta hai. Agar order lagte waqt Limit Order ka bhav `Target_Premium_Range` ke bahar jaaye, toh bot order nahi lagata `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: aaj ki range strict hai, aur slippage buffer range ke andar hi ginta hai.

Agar Limit Order `Entry_Order_Cancel_Samay` mein fill na ho, toh bot use cancel kar deta hai. Yeh samay broker ke order ko manjoor karne se ginta hai, bhejne se nahi `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Agar cancel ke waqt tak order fill ho chuka ho, toh use fill hi maana jaata hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Woh chhoote hue bhav ke peeche bhaag kar dobara mehenga order nahi lagata. Wajah: bhav ke peeche bhaagna Late Entry ka nuksan deta hai.

Agar Lot ki sankhya freeze limit ya Market Depth se badi ho, toh bot Order Slicing karta hai. 1 Lot ka order tukdon mein nahi bantta; depth kam ho toh strike chhoda jaata hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: khud ke bade order se bazar ka bhav na uchhle. Alag-alag reject par bot kya karta hai, yeh Pehredaar aur Suraksha mein hai. Agar order `Order_Reject_Ginti` baar reject ho, toh bot naye order band kar deta hai.

Agar order aadha fill ho, toh bot bacha hua hissa cancel kar deta hai. Jitna fill hua, agar woh kam se kam `Minimum_Trade_Lot` ho, use asli khula trade maana jaata hai. Us par Broker Stop lagta hai (dekho: Pehredaar aur Suraksha). Bot usi Signal ID par dobara order nahi lagata aur khule trade ki details Dashboard ko bhejta hai. Wajah: ek hi signal par do baar trade na lag jaaye.

Din mein auto-entries ki upari seema hoti hai. Agar din mein `Daily_Max_Auto_Entries` poore ho jaayein ya `Aakhri_Entry_Samay` baj jaaye, toh naye entry band ho jaate hain. Wajah: whipsaw wale din bot overtrading na kare. Bot ek index mein `Prati_Index_Max_Open_Trade` aur kul milakar `Kul_Max_Open_Trades` hi khule rakhta hai. Wajah: teeno indices saath chalne se zyada khule trades ek hi bade daon ban jaate hain.

Trade band hone ke baad cooldown lagta hai. Agar pichla trade nuksan mein band hua ho, toh bot `Loss_Cooldown_Samay` rukta hai aur naye breakout par hi enter karta hai. Naya breakout woh candle hai jis par `Range_Breakout_Check` pehli baar dobara GREEN ho `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: badla lene wali jaldbaazi (revenge trading) na ho. Agar pichla trade munafe mein band hua ho, toh bot sirf `Profit_Cooldown_Samay` rukta hai. Wajah: chalte trend ka agla mauka na chhoote.

Agar ek din mein `Lagatar_Loss_Din_Band_Ginti` trades nuksan mein band hon, toh us din naye entry band ho jaate hain `[TAKRAAV #4]`. Nuksan matlab kharche ke baad net nuksan; breakeven ya chhote fayde mein kata trade is ginti ko todta hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: lagatar nuksan batata hai ki aaj bazaar anukool nahi hai. `Lagatar_Ulte_Auto_Entries_Ginti` mein woh entry ginti hain jo `Time_Stop_Samay` ke andar Stop-Loss ya breakeven par kat gayi. Yeh ginti din badalne par bhi chalti hai aur pehle fayde wale trade par ruk jaati hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: do nuksan par din band ho jaata hai, isliye yeh ginti din ke bheetar poori nahi ho sakti.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-13 | High]` Dikkat: Khule Trade ki ginti mein pending entry order aur chal raha exit gine jaate hain ya nahi, yeh likha nahi. Isse `Prati_Index_Max_Open_Trade` aur `Kul_Max_Open_Trades` toot sakte hain. Agar kisi index mein position, pending entry order ya chal raha exit ho, toh woh us index ka Khula Trade gina jaaye (dekho: Naam-Suchi). Wajah: order ke beech ke samay mein doosra entry na ghuse. Asar: dohre jokhim ka rasta band.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Hypothesis H-02: ask se upar `Entry_Order_Slippage_Buffer` wala Limit Order har baar poora spread deta hai. Test: Paper Stage aur Stage2 mein "bid aur ask ke beech se shuru karke ek baar sudhaar" wale order ka fill-dar aur Slippage milao. Agar fill-dar `Entry_Fill_Dar_Min` se upar rahe aur Slippage ghate, toh malik ki manzoori se badlaav socha jaaye. Wajah: har trade ka chhota Slippage saal bhar mein bada kharcha banta hai.

## 11. Exit

Har khule trade ka Stop-Loss entry ke saath hi tay ho jaata hai (dekho: Pehredaar aur Suraksha). Pehla Stop-Loss premium ke ATR (dekho: Tayyar Code (Repo)) aur swing low ke hisaab se lagta hai. ATR `ATR_Avadhi` ki avadhi ka aur Stop-Loss ki doori `ATR_Stop_Guna` guna hoti hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Ismein entry bhav se `Premium_Max_Stop_Loss_Percent` ki girawat ek aakhri upari hadd hoti hai. Wajah: shant bazaar mein chhota Stop-Loss lage aur tez bazaar mein normal halchal se stop na ude.

Doosra Stop-Loss index ke chart ka hota hai. Agar index breakout level ke wapas andar aakar `Index_Stop_Tikav_Samay` tak tik jaaye, toh trade band ho jaata hai. Dono Stop-Loss mein se jo pehle chale, wahi trade band karta hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: breakout fail hone ke baad option rakhne ka matlab nahi hai.

Jab trade munafe mein jaata hai, toh bot munafa lock karta hai. Agar premium `Breakeven_Shift_Profit_Percent` badh jaaye, toh Stop-Loss kharcha nikaal kar breakeven par aa jaata hai. Wajah: ab us trade mein jeb se nuksan nahi hoga. Trailing Stop-Loss premium ke sabse oonche bhav se `Trailing_Stop_Doori` neeche chalta hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Har baar Stop-Loss khiskane par Broker Stop bhi khisakta hai (dekho: Pehredaar aur Suraksha).

Agar premium `Partial_Profit_Booking_Percent` badh jaaye aur trade mein `Partial_Exit_Minimum_Lots` hon, toh bot aadhe Lot bech deta hai. Taak Lot ho toh aadhe se kam, yaani neeche round hokar, bechta hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Baaki bache Lot ko woh Trailing Stop-Loss par chhod deta hai. Wajah: kuch munafa pakka ho jaaye aur baaki se bada trend mile. Agar trade mein sirf `Minimum_Trade_Lot` ho, toh bot use bechne ke bajay Trailing Stop-Loss kas deta hai. Wajah: ek Lot aadha nahi bik sakta, par aaya hua munafa bachana zaroori hai.

Waqt aur entry ki wajah se bhi exit hota hai. Agar `Time_Stop_Samay` mein bhi premium `Time_Stop_Chaal_Paimana` se zyada na hile, toh bot Time-Stop se trade nikaal deta hai. Wajah: atke hue trade mein Theta Decay chupke se paisa kha jaata hai. Agar entry wale bade checks `Ulta_Check_Tikav_Samay` tak ulte rahein (jaise Breadth bigde, Futures discount ya SUSPICIOUS flag), toh bot nikaal deta hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: entry ka kaaran khatam toh trade bhi khatam, par ek pal ke jhatke par exit na ho. Data atakne ya circuit par exit ka raasta Pehredaar aur Suraksha mein hai.

Har samay aur khatre ke exit mein khule trade ko band hi kiya jaata hai, Stop-Loss tight karke nahi rakha jaata `[TAKRAAV #9]` `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Event se `Event_Exit_Pehle_Samay` pehle, Shock_Mode lagte hi, aur `Expiry_Shift_Aur_Exit_Samay` par expiry wala current trade band hota hai. `Auto_Square_Off_Samay` par saare trades band hote hain. `Daily_Loss_Risk_Limit` toote ya Kill-Switch dabe, toh bhi khule trades nikaale jaate hain. `Tight_Stop_Doori` ab kisi exit mein nahi lagta. Wajah: koi bhi trade khatre mein ya raat ko khula na rahe.

Exit ke waqt bot intezaar nahi karta. Agar trade band karna ho, toh bot pehle Broker Stop cancel karke uska confirm leta hai (dekho: Pehredaar aur Suraksha). Phir woh chalte bid bhav se `Aggressive_Exit_Bid_Offset` neeche Aggressive Limit Order lagata hai. Agar woh `Exit_Order_Update_Samay` mein fill na ho, toh bot bhav badal kar dobara bhejta hai, aur `Exit_Reprice_Max_Ginti` ke baad MPP Order se nikalta hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: nikalte waqt har haal mein turant fill zaroori hai. `Auto_Square_Off_Samay` ek hi samay hai aur broker ke apne square-off se pehle rakha jaata hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-22 | High]` Dikkat: pehla Stop-Loss ATR aur swing low dono se banta hai, par dono alag hon toh kaunsa chune, yeh likha nahi. Agar dono alag hon, toh jo entry se zyada door ho woh liya jaaye, kyunki Swing Low ke upar ka stop aam halchal mein kat-ta hai. Malik ke niyam mein yeh doori `Premium_Max_Stop_Loss_Percent` par ruk jaati hai. Wajah: PRD wala AI do stop mein se apni marzi se na chune.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Hypothesis H-06 (vikalp): agar sahi jagah ka stop `Premium_Max_Stop_Loss_Percent` se door ho, toh stop ko paas khinchne ke bajay trade liya hi na jaaye. Test: Backtest mein "cap par stop" banam "trade skip" ki Net Expectancy milao. Malik ka niyam test hone tak waisa hi rahega. Wajah: jabardasti paas rakha stop aam halchal mein kat kar nuksan ki ginti badhata hai.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-20 | High]` `[TAKRAAV #30]` Dikkat: `Trailing_Stop_Doori` pehle Stop-Loss ki doori (`ATR_Stop_Guna`) se chhoti hai, aur trailing kab shuru ho, yeh likha nahi. Agar trailing entry se hi chale, toh pehla Stop-Loss turant paas aa jaayega. Agar Stop-Loss breakeven par aa chuka ho, tabhi Trailing Stop-Loss shuru ho. Wajah: trade ko pehle saans lene ki jagah mile. Asar: jaldi kate trade kam.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-21 | High]` Dikkat: bot ka Stop-Loss kis bhav par chale (aakhri saude ka bhav, bid ya beech ka bhav), yeh tay nahi. Ek akela galat saude ka bhav ya pal bhar ka chauda spread stop ko chala sakta hai. Agar best bid lagatar `Stop_Pushti_Ginti` updates tak Stop-Loss par ya usse neeche rahe, toh Stop-Loss chale. Broker Stop exchange ke apne niyam se chalta hai. Wajah: bot wahi bhav dekhe jis par woh asal mein bech sakta hai.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-46 | Med]` "Trailing Stop-Loss kas deta hai" ki nayi doori `Trailing_Kasi_Doori` hai. Wajah: bina number ke yeh niyam test nahi ho sakta.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-61 | Med]` Agar exit ka Aggressive Limit Order aadha fill ho, toh bacha hissa usi kram mein bhav badal kar dobara bheja jaaye. Har fill ke baad Broker Stop ki quantity bache Lot ke barabar ki jaaye. Wajah: aadha band trade bina Stop ke na rahe.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Hypothesis H-05: munafa percent ke bajay Stop-Unit ke guna par liya jaaye (jaise pehla hissa ek Stop-Unit ke barabar munafe par). Hypothesis H-07: Time-Stop expiry tak ke bache din aur theta ke hisaab se badle. Test: Backtest mein dono ki Net Expectancy aur girawat malik ke niyam se milao. Wajah: nikalne ke niyam hi asli kamai tay karte hain.

## 12. Ek udaharan

Nakli udaharan. Number sirf samjhane ke liye, niyam nahi.

Subah 8:45 baje malik phone par bot aur Pehredaar ke Upstox login ki manzoori deta hai. Bot subah ki taiyari jaanchta hai. Broker login, order ka raasta, Static IP, live data, Instrument Master, ghadi ka milaan, 252 din ka IV record aur dividend check theek milte hain. Bot pichli closing ke bhav se 1 Lot ka anumaan lagakar Fund Request bhejta hai. Malik paisa daalkar Real Money Mode chunta hai aur confirm karke Start Control dabata hai. Pehredaar alag app aur alag machine par chalu hota hai.

9:15 se 9:30 tak (15 minute) bot sirf dekhta hai aur koi trade nahi leta. 9:15 ke baad ke asli bhav se woh `Target_Premium_Range` tay karta hai. 9:30 baje ADX 28 aur opening range se Din ka Mausam "Trending" tay hota hai (`Din_Ka_Mausam_Gate` GREEN).

Subah 10:15 baje Momentum Zone chal raha hai (`Trading_Zone_Gate` GREEN), koi news nahi hai (`Bade_Event_Lock` GREEN) aur `Shock_Mode` GREEN hai. Bot Nifty50 mein 0.52 Delta wala Call strike (bhav 200 rupaye) shortlist karta hai (`Delta_Filter` GREEN). Premium 200 rupaye `Target_Premium_Range` ke andar hai (`Premium_Range_Gate` GREEN) aur account mein 1 Lot ka poora paisa hai (`Balance_Ek_Lot_Gate` GREEN). Strike `Expiry_Filter`, `IV_Overpriced_Filter` aur `IVP_Filter` (IVP 24 percent) pass kar leta hai. Top 5 levels mein Slippage 1.2 percent hai (`Liquidity_Filter` GREEN). Agli deewar tak fayda kharche se 4 guna (`Kharcha_Filter` GREEN) aur volume 1.8 guna (`Strike_Volume_Filter` GREEN) hai.

Order-Flow mein `Premium_Tezi_Check` (2 points), `Volume_Uchhal_Check` (2 points, Futures volume 3.2 guna, VWAP upar) aur `Trap_Detection_Check` (3 points) GREEN hain. `Rate_Update_Gati_Check` Shadow Mode mein chalta hai aur score mein nahi ginta. Aas-paas ke 4 strikes bhaag rahe hain (`Fake_Spike_Filter` GREEN) aur premium 8 percent hi badha hai (`Chase_Guard` GREEN). Technical mein 30 minute `Range_Breakout_Check` (3 points), 15 va 3 minute `Timeframe_Match_Check` (3 points), `EMA_Slope_Check` (1 point) aur `RSI_Momentum_Check` (1 point, RSI 64) GREEN hain. Directional-Bias mein `OI_Deewar_Short_Covering_Check` (5 points) aur `Teeno_Index_Disha_Check` (3 points) GREEN hain; `OI_Deewar_Doori_Gate` bhi GREEN hai. Sentiment mein `Index_Breadth_Check` (5 points), `Heavyweight_Stocks_Check` (3 points) aur `VIX_Gati_Term_Structure_Check` (2 points) GREEN hain.

`Manipulation_Cash_Volume_Check` aur `Manipulation_Settlement_Check` GREEN hain; `Manipulation_GEX_Mausam_Check` Shadow Mode mein hai. `Lambi_Wick_Check`, `Tick_Heartbeat_Check` (120 ms), `Broker_Exchange_Gadbad_Check` aur `Call_Put_Takraav_Gate` bhi GREEN hain. Chaaron groups mein GREEN hai aur kul 12 Score checks GREEN hain. Zaroori score 10 hai, isliye har group ka cap 5 points hai. Order-Flow ke 7, Technical ke 8, Directional-Bias ke 8 aur Sentiment ke 10 points cap hokar 5-5 rah jaate hain, aur kul score 20 points hota hai.

ATR Stop-Loss 160 rupaye par tay hota hai. Balance mein bas 1 Lot ka paisa hai, isliye bot Intent Log likhkar 1 Lot ka Limit Order lagata hai (adhikatam 202 rupaye). Woh 201 rupaye par 1 second mein fill hota hai. Fill ke 2 second ke andar Broker Stop lag jaata hai (trigger 150 rupaye, limit bhav 145.5 rupaye).

10:28 baje premium 35 percent badh kar 271 rupaye hota hai. Bot apna Stop-Loss aur Broker Stop khiskakar breakeven (203 rupaye) par le aata hai, aur har khiskaav ka broker se confirm leta hai. 10:36 baje premium 55 percent badh kar 311 rupaye hota hai, toh bot Trailing Stop-Loss kas deta hai, kyunki trade mein sirf 1 Lot hai. 10:48 baje exit ke waqt bot pehle Broker Stop cancel karta hai aur broker ka confirm leta hai. Phir woh trade 295 rupaye par Aggressive Limit Order se bechta hai aur net quantity zero milti hai.

Phir bot 5 minute Profit Cooldown lagata hai aur kundali Dashboard par dikhata hai. Usi din BankNifty mein 310 rupaye ka tagada premium dikha jo range ke bahar tha. Bot ne use nahi kharida (`Premium_Range_Gate` RED) aur Telegram par Range Alert bheja.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` **Doosra nakli udaharan (galti wala din).** Nakli udaharan. Number sirf samjhane ke liye, niyam nahi.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` 11:05 baje BankNifty mein bot ka 2 Lot ka Put khula hai, Broker Stop laga hai. Bot ke machine par 5 second ka atkaav aata hai. 4 second par bot ka Lease khatam hota hai, toh bot broker ko koi hukm nahi bhejta. 6 second par Pehredaar "Pehredaar ne control liya" nishan likhta hai, Broker Stop cancel karta hai, confirm leta hai, aur broker ki net quantity padhta hai. Broker 1 Lot dikhata hai, kyunki Broker Stop pehle hi 1 Lot par fill ho chuka tha; Pehredaar sirf 1 Lot bechta hai aur net quantity zero milti hai.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Bot wapas chalta hai, nishan padhta hai aur us din broker ko koi hukm nahi bhejta. Malik ko Critical Alert Pehredaar ke apne rasta se jaata hai. Shaam ko malik Kill-Switch dabata hai toh Kill Latch lagta hai; agle din malik ke Start Control tak bot naya entry nahi leta.

## 13. Dashboard

Dashboard ek alag live website hai. Yeh dikhata hai ki bot kya kar raha hai aur kyun. Malik isi website se Mode Chunav, Start aur Stop karta hai. Trade ka faisla hamesha bot hi leta hai, Dashboard nahi (dekho: Tayyar Code (Repo)). Dashboard mein bot ka koi niyam, check ka vazan ya Risk Limit badalne ka rasta nahi hota.

Dashboard sirf malik dekhta hai. Pehle login hota hai aur Real Money Mode ke Controls par ek doosra step lagta hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: Dashboard internet par khula hota hai aur Controls se asli paisa chalta hai. Dashboard ke paas broker ya Telegram ki chaabi ka koi rasta nahi hota (dekho: Pehredaar aur Suraksha).

Malik website kholkar do mein se ek mode chunta hai: Paper Mode ya Real Money Mode. Malik dono mein se koi bhi chun sakta hai. Mode chalte bot mein nahi badalta aur dono mode ek saath nahi chalte `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Agar malik Paper Mode chunkar Start Control dabaye, toh bot kaagaz par bina asli paise ke chalta hai. Wajah: tay nahi.

Agar malik Real Money Mode chunkar Start Control dabaye, toh bot Upstox account mein asli paise se asli order lagata hai. Wajah: tay nahi. Real Money Mode ka Start Control tabhi chalta hai jab bot aur Pehredaar dono ka token mil chuka ho (dekho: Bot ka ek poora din). Agar malik Stop Control dabaye, toh naye entry band hote hain, khule trades apne exit se band hote hain aur phir bot rukta hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: tay nahi.

Kill-Switch, Stop Control aur Real Money Mode ka Start Control dabane par ek confirm step aata hai. Agar `Control_Confirm_Samay` mein confirm na ho, toh hukm bot ko nahi jaata `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: galti se ya galat haath se asli paise ka kaam na ho.

Dashboard ke sabse upar ki patti mein bot ki live haalat dikhti hai. Wahan bot ka mode (Paper Mode ya Real Money Mode; Real Money Mode mein Stage ke hisaab se Lot aur Stage Override ka nishan), bot Start hai ya Stop, Din ka Mausam aur chal raha zone dikhta hai. Wahin aaj ke bache hue entry, aaj ke nuksan ka hissa aur Daily Profit Lock dikhta hai. Koi Gate active ho (Shock Mode, Bade_Event_Lock, order reject flag ya ooncha IV sandesh) toh woh bhi upar dikhta hai. Wahin ek red warning-light batati hai ki bot purane data ki wajah se pause hai, crash nahi hua.

Patti par "Taiyari Fail" (wajah ki suchi ke saath), "Paisa Kam", "Range Mein Strike Nahi" aur "Malik ne roka" ke nishan bhi dikhte hain `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Fund Request aur Foreign Position bhi wahin dikhte hain. Har dabbe ke saath uske data ki umar dikhti hai, taaki purana data taaza na lage. Dashboard `Dashboard_Refresh_Samay` par naya data dikhata hai.

Upar ki patti mein hi Mode Chunav, Start Control aur Stop Control ke saath suraksha ke do Controls (buttons) rehte hain: Emergency Pause aur Kill-Switch. Agar Emergency Pause dabaya jaaye, toh bot sirf naye entry rokta hai aur khule trades chalne deta hai. Wajah: naya jokhim rokte hue chalte trade ko niyam se chalne dena. Agar Kill-Switch dabaya jaaye, toh pending orders cancel hote hain aur khule trades turant band hote hain; hukm seedha Pehredaar ke Exit Hissa ko jaata hai (dekho: Pehredaar aur Suraksha). Wajah: aapat sthiti mein poora jokhim turant khatam karna (malik Upstox app se haath se bhi band kar sakta hai).

Upar ki patti ke neeche har check ka apna chhota live dabba hota hai `[TAKRAAV #19]`. Yeh dabbe har index ke liye aur Call va Put side ke liye alag-alag GREEN, RED ya NA dikhate hain. Iske neeche khule trades ki table hoti hai: index, strike, Call/Put, Lot, samay, live bhav, net fayda-nuksan aur Stop-Loss.

Uske neeche band ho chuke trades ki history aur unka final bhav hota hai. Har band trade ke saath uske band hone ki wajah (Stop-Loss, profit lock, Time-Stop, Kill-Switch ya haath se band) likhi hoti hai. Kisi bhi trade par click karne se uski poori kundali ka panel khulta hai. Kundali mein step-by-step dikhta hai ki Din ka Mausam, Order-Flow, Directional-Bias aur manipulation check ne kya bola tha.

Dashboard par Fund Request bhi dikhti hai: aaj kitna paisa chahiye. Dashboard par Range Alert ki poori detail bhi achhe se dikhti hai: strike, premium aur balance ka haal `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Telegram par sirf chhota alert jaata hai. Wajah: user ne kaha Telegram par khali alert aur website par achhe se. Reject wajah mein "Premium Out Of Range" aur "Balance Kam" bhi dikhte hain.

Dashboard par reject hue strikes aur unki wajah ("Low Liquidity", "Fake Spike", "Late Entry", "OI Wall Too Close", "SUSPICIOUS") bhi dikhti hai. Order reject ho ya aadha fill ho, toh woh bhi Dashboard par dikhta hai. Bot se Dashboard ko live haalat, checks status, khule-band trades, kundali, reject strikes aur order alerts ka data jaata hai. Dashboard se bot ko Mode Chunav, Start Control, Stop Control aur Emergency Pause ka hukm jaata hai. Kill-Switch ka hukm Pehredaar ke Exit Hissa ko jaata hai.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-17 | High]` Dikkat: Dashboard internet par khula hai, aur usse bot aur Pehredaar ko hukm jaate hain. Dashboard par kabza ho jaaye toh nakli hukm ja sakta hai. Har hukm par malik ki pehchaan, hukm ka samay aur ek baar ka alag number ho; `Command_Max_Umar` se purana ya dohraya hukm bot aur Pehredaar na maanein. Bot aur Pehredaar Dashboard ke liye koi khula darwaza nahi rakhte; woh hukm khud lene jaate hain, aur Pehredaar Dashboard se sirf Kill-Switch maanta hai. Wajah: Dashboard ki galti asli paise tak na pahunche.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-17 | High]` Har hukm ka record rahe: kisne, kab, kis jagah se, aur bot ne kya kiya. Wajah: baad mein dikhe ki kaun sa hukm asli tha.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-47 | Med]` Dikkat: Dashboard band ho jaaye toh malik Start, Stop ya Kill-Switch kaise dabaye, yeh tay nahi. Agar bot `Dashboard_Band_Hadd` tak Dashboard se jud na paaye, toh naye entry band hon, khule trade apne Exit niyam se chalein, aur malik ko Zaroori Alert jaaye. Aapat haalat mein malik Upstox app se trade kaat sakta hai; doosra aapat rasta Sawal 129 mein hai. Wajah: malik ke control ke bina bot ko naya jokhim nahi lena chahiye.

## 14. Alerts aur insaan ki bhoomika

Bot kuch khaas mauko par malik ke phone par turant alert bhejta hai. Agar taiyari kharab ho, order fail ho, bot pause ho, Pehredaar chale ya Risk Limit toote, toh phone alert jaata hai. Wajah: har gambhir sthiti ki khabar malik ko turant milni chahiye. Phone alert Telegram par aata hai. Telegram par sirf alert aata hai aur poori detail Dashboard par dikhti hai. Wajah: user ne kaha Telegram par khali alert aur website par achhe se.

Alert ke teen darje hain `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Critical Alert: Pehredaar chale, Broker Stop na lage, Kill-Switch fail ho, khule trade ka bhav na mile, order ka raasta kharab ho ya broker va exchange ruke. Zaroori Alert: taiyari fail, order reject, bot pause, Risk Limit toote, Foreign Position mile aur roz subah ke login ki manzoori. Jaankari Alert: Fund Request, Range Alert aur trade band hone ki khabar. Wajah: har alert par malik ka kaam alag hota hai.

Critical Alert Telegram ke saath email par bhi jaata hai. Agar Telegram na pahunche, toh bot `Alert_Dobara_Bhejne_Ginti` baar dobara bhejta hai, phir email aur Dashboard par dikhata hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: gambhir khabar ek hi raaste par atak na jaaye. Telegram par koi Yes ya Approve button nahi hota.

Subah bot Fund Request bhejta hai ki account mein itna paisa daalo. Wajah: user ne kaha bot pehle hi bataye ki kitna balance chahiye.

Agar koi premium `Target_Premium_Range` ke bahar ho par bahut tagada dikhe, toh bot Telegram par Range Alert bhejta hai `[TAKRAAV #22]`. Wajah: aisa mauka malik ki nazar se chhoot na jaaye. Premium tabhi Tagada Premium maana jaata hai jab woh `Range_Alert_Upari_Premium` ke andar ho aur baaki saare Gates aur Scorecard pass kare `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Bot us premium ko khud nahi kharidta. Wajah: `Target_Premium_Range` strict hai.

Agar balance kam ho, toh Range Alert mein bataya jaata hai ki balance badhana hoga. Alert mein koi Yes ya Approve button nahi hota. Wajah: user ne kaha Telegram par khali alert. Range Alert ke baad malik Upstox app se khud khareed sakta hai; website par uska button nahi hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: bot ka apna entry sirf scorecard se hota hai.

Range Alert ke beech `Alert_Cooldown_Samay` ka gap rehta hai, din mein zyada se zyada `Daily_Max_Alerts` jaate hain aur `Alert_Aakhri_Samay` ke baad nahi jaate `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: alert se overtrading ka lalach wapas na aaye. Entry ke baad exit hamesha bot khud karta hai (dekho: Exit). Wajah: Telegram par Close dabane ki zaroorat nahi.

Critical Alert par malik Emergency Pause ya Kill-Switch daba sakta hai ya Upstox app se trade kaat sakta hai. Agar `Alert_Jawab_Samay` tak jawab na aaye, toh bot aur Pehredaar apne suraksha niyam aur exit khud chalate rehte hain `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: malik na ho tab bhi suraksha na ruke. Malik khaas din par manzoori de sakta hai `[TAKRAAV #1]` aur `Ooncha_IV_Avadhi` tak ooncha IV rehne par limit soch sakta hai.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-16 | High]` Dikkat: alert bot bhejta hai, par bot hi ruk jaaye toh "Pehredaar chale" jaisa alert kaun bhejega? Pehredaar ka apna alert rasta ho (apna Telegram aur email), bot se alag, aur uski chaabi sirf Pehredaar ki machine ke surakshit store mein ho. Bot Pehredaar ki dhadkan dekhe; agar Pehredaar `Pehredaar_Dhadkan_Chhoot_Ginti` tak chup rahe, toh bot naye entry band kare aur Critical Alert bheje. Agar dono chup hon, toh Dashboard par laal nishan aur email alert jaaye. Wajah: chaukidar ki bhi nigrani zaroori hai.

## 15. Report, Record aur Niyam badalna

Bot har din ke ant mein apni ek poori report banata hai. Ismein kul signals, reject strikes va unki wajah, kul trades, Slippage, kharcha aur har check ka sahi-galat nateeja hota hai. Wajah: roz ke kaam ka saaf hisaab saamne rahe. Shadow Mode ke checks aur "Sideways Din" tag wale din bhi report mein alag dikhte hain `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: Shadow Mode ke checks ka asar jaanch ke baad hi tay hoga.

Reject kiye hue signal ka baad mein kya hua, bot yeh bhi record karta hai. Woh nateeja `Reject_Signal_Nateeja_Samay` baad ek baar, bid aur ask ke beech ke bhav se naapta hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: pata chale ki bot ne sahi trade chhoda ya galat.

Checks ke vazan aur limits (jaise IVP ya Delta range) bot kabhi apne aap nahi badalta. `Niyam_Badalne_Nateeje_Avadhi` ke asli nateeje dekhkar insaan hi unhe haath se badalta hai. Wajah: thode dino ke nateeje se khud ko badalna galat seekh de sakta hai. Insaan niyam ek alag niyam-file mein badalta hai aur har badlaav ka record rehta hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: kaun si limit kab badli, yeh baad mein dikhna chahiye.

Report, trades aur niyam-badlaav ka record `Record_Rakhne_Avadhi` tak rakha jaata hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Tax ke liye roz ki alag file bhi banti hai. Wajah: record kitne saal rakhna hai, yeh tax salahkaar se pushti hona baaki hai.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-49 | Med]` Bot har din ka Replay Record rakhe: har aaya data (tick, Option Chain, depth, order ka jawab) apne samay ke saath, aur har faisla. Agar wahi din dobara chalaya jaaye, toh wahi faisle nikalne chahiye; na niklein toh yeh galti maani jaaye. Backtest, Paper Mode aur Real Money Mode mein faisle ka ek hi niyam chale. Wajah: galti dobara dekhi ja sake aur kaagaz ka nateeja asli se mel khaye.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-48 | Med]` Reject hue signal ka nateeja `Reject_Signal_Nateeja_Samay` par naapne ke saath, Shadow Trade se bhi naapa jaaye: wahi Limit Order, wahi Stop-Loss, wahi exit niyam aur wahi kharcha. Wajah: ek pal ka bhav reject ko sahi ya galat dikha sakta hai; Shadow Trade asli trade jaisa hisaab deta hai. Asar: Gate aur vazan badalne ka faisla behtar data par hoga.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Agar koi niyam ya vazan badalna ho, toh naya niyam Backtest ke Out-of-Sample hisse aur Paper Mode dono mein parkha jaaye. Har badlaav ke saath likha jaaye ki kitne vikalp aazmaaye gaye the. Wajah: bahut vikalp aazmaane par koi ek kismat se achha dikh sakta hai.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Agar Shadow Mode ka check `Shadow_Se_Active_Min_Signal` signals tak chale, aur Ablation mein uske jodne se Net Expectancy ka nichla Confidence Interval upar khiske, tabhi malik use Active kare. Wajah: check data se apni jagah kamaaye, umeed se nahi.

## 16. Test aur Live par jaane ka plan

Bot ko live paise par chalane se pehle teen Stage mein uski kadi parakh hoti hai: Paper Stage, Stage2 aur Poora Lot Stage. Pehle, Paper Mode se pehle, bot purane bazaar data par Backtest se parakha jaata hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Dashboard par malik Paper Mode ya Real Money Mode koi bhi chun sakta hai, isliye Stage aur Mode Chunav kaise milenge, yeh tay nahi hua `[TAKRAAV #12]`. Agar malik Paper Stage se pehle Real Money Mode chune, toh bot Stage2 ke Lot par chalta hai aur Dashboard par Stage Override dikhta hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`.

Paper Stage mein bot `Paper_Mode_Avadhi` tak Paper Mode mein bina asli paise ke chalta hai (dekho: Tayyar Code (Repo)). Paper Mode ka balance `Paper_Mode_Balance` hota hai aur uska record alag rakha jaata hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Paper Mode mein bhi woh asli Market Depth, Slippage aur saare tax-brokerage jodkar hisaab lagata hai. Woh har signal, reject strike aur trade ka nateeja likhta hai. Wajah: kaagaz ka nateeja asli bazaar ke kareeb ho.

Stage2 mein bot asli bazaar mein `Test_Stage2_Live_Lot` se `Stage2_Avadhi` tak chalta hai aur kaagaz ko asli fill se milata hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Wajah: asli order par Slippage ki sacchai saamne aati hai. Agar Paper aur asli fill ka farak `Paper_Asli_Fill_Farak_Hadd` se zyada ho, toh Stage badalne ka paimana poora nahi maana jaata. Teesre Stage mein nateeje bharose ke hone par bot poore Lot size par aata hai.

Agle Stage par jaane ka paimana `Stage_Badlaav_Paimana` hai `[PRASTAAVIT]`. Paper Stage mein kam se kam `Paper_Mode_Trade_Ginti` trades hone chahiye. Agar `Trade_Kam_Din_Ginti` wale din bahut kam trades hon, toh bot ki trade ginti jaanchi jaati hai. Niyam ya limit badalne par Stage dobara shuru nahi hota.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-19 | High]` Dikkat: Stage badalne ka paimana "kul nateeja nuksan mein nahi" hai, aur trade ginti kam hai. Itne kam trade mein kismat aur hunar alag nahi dikhte. Har Stage ka paimana Net Expectancy ke Confidence Interval se ho, sirf ausat se nahi (`Backtest_Min_Trade_Ginti`, `Paper_Gate_CC`, `Stage2_Gate_CC`). Wajah: ausat positive hona sabit karna nahi hai.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Backtest Gate: Backtest Walk-Forward ho, kam se kam `Backtest_Min_Avadhi` ka, aur aakhri hissa Out-of-Sample ho. Agar Out-of-Sample mein `Backtest_Min_Trade_Ginti` trade na hon, `Expectancy_CI_Star` poora na ho, `Profit_Factor_Min` na mile ya `Parameter_Stability_Paimana` fail ho, toh Paper Stage shuru nahi hota. Wajah: jo purane data par kharche ke baad nahi kamata, woh asli bazaar mein bhi shayad nahi kamayega.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-18 | High]` `[TAKRAAV #35]` Dikkat: malik Paper Stage se pehle Real Money Mode chun sakta hai, isliye poori parakh ek click se chhoot sakti hai. Agar Backtest Gate pass na hua ho, toh Real Money Mode ka Start Control na chale. Agar malik Stage Override chune, toh bot sirf `Stage_Override_Max_Lot` par chale aur malik likh kar pushti de. Wajah: parakh ka niyam ek galti se na toote. Asar: shuruaati nuksan ka sabse bada rasta band.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Live band karne ki kasauti: agar Real Money Mode mein `Live_Kill_Paimana` ki koi bhi shart sach ho, toh bot naye entry band kare. Malik ki jaanch aur likhit pushti tak Real Money Mode wapas na chale. Shartein hain: bahut gehri girawat, Net Expectancy ka Backtest se bahut neeche aana, Slippage ka bahut badhna, ya kisi Critical niyam ka tootna. Wajah: bura daur aur toota bot, dono jaldi pakde jaayein.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-52 | Med]` Paper Mode ka fill niyam tay nahi. Agar Paper Mode ka Limit Order lage, toh fill tabhi maana jaaye jab saamne ki depth mein us bhav par poori quantity ho. Fill ka bhav depth mein chal kar nikle, aur order aur fill ke beech `Paper_Deri_Model` jitni deri jode. Wajah: kaagaz ke fill asli se achhe dikhenge toh Stage ka paimana dhokha dega.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Stage2 se pehle saare fault test pass hon (suchi: `04_Build_Roadmap.md`). Inmein Pehredaar ka control lena, Broker Stop ka aadha fill, Kill Latch, token band, feed atakna aur dohra order shaamil hain. `Stage_Badlaav_Paimana` mein "Broker Stop va Pehredaar ke saare test paas" ka matlab yahi suchi ho. Wajah: suraksha ke niyam asli paise se pehle sabit hon.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `[F-67 | Med]` Broker ka sandbox sirf order ka dhaancha jaanchta hai; woh asli Market Depth ya fill nahi deta (dekho: Tarikh wali baatein). Isliye Paper Mode bot ka apna hoga, asli live data aur `Paper_Deri_Model` ke saath (Sawal 116 ka prastaavit jawab). Wajah: sandbox ka fill Stage ke paimane ke liye bekaar hai.

## 17. Tayyar Code (Repo)

Pehla repo: `bukosabino/ta` ([https://github.com/bukosabino/ta](https://github.com/bukosabino/ta)). Kaam: Section 3 (ADX) aur Section 7 va 11 (EMA, RSI, Bollinger Bands, Keltner Channel, VWAP, ATR). Zaroori file: `ta/trend.py` ([https://github.com/bukosabino/ta/blob/master/ta/trend.py](https://github.com/bukosabino/ta/blob/master/ta/trend.py)), `ta/momentum.py` ([https://github.com/bukosabino/ta/blob/master/ta/momentum.py](https://github.com/bukosabino/ta/blob/master/ta/momentum.py)), `ta/volatility.py` ([https://github.com/bukosabino/ta/blob/master/ta/volatility.py](https://github.com/bukosabino/ta/blob/master/ta/volatility.py)) aur `ta/volume.py` ([https://github.com/bukosabino/ta/blob/master/ta/volume.py](https://github.com/bukosabino/ta/blob/master/ta/volume.py)). License: MIT License. Maalik: Darío López Padial (`bukosabino`, vyakti).

Is pehle repo ki aakhri update March 2026 (README 8 mahine pehle, `ta/` files 3 saal pehle) hai. Kitne log use karte hain: 5.2k stars, 1.2k forks, 10k+ projects. Jaanch ki tarikh: 7 Nov 2026. Kya dekha: `ta/trend.py` aur `ta/momentum.py` khol kar padha, unmein sirf ganit ka hisaab hai aur koi baahari call nahi hai. Farak: `ta/volume.py` ka VWAP rolling `window` maangta hai, jabki idea mein din-bhar ka VWAP chahiye `[TAKRAAV #24]`. Dobara jaanch 9 Nov 2026: `ta/volume.py` aur `ta/volatility.py` poori khol kar padhin. Unmein sirf numpy aur pandas ke import aur bhav ka ganit hai, network, file ya chaabi ka koi call nahi dikha. VWAP ka hisaab rolling `window` par hi chalta hai, yaani pehle se likha farak sahi nikla.

Doosra repo: `joshyattridge/smart-money-concepts` ([https://github.com/joshyattridge/smart-money-concepts](https://github.com/joshyattridge/smart-money-concepts)). Kaam: Section 7 (`Order_Block_BOS_CHoCH_FVG_Check` ke liye Order Blocks, BOS, CHoCH aur Fair Value Gap). Zaroori file: `smartmoneyconcepts/smc.py`. License: MIT License. Maalik: Joshua Attridge (`joshyattridge`, vyakti).

Is doosre repo ki aakhri update March 2026 (8 mahine pehle, version 0.0.27) hai. Kitne log use karte hain: 2.1k stars, 895 forks. Jaanch ki tarikh: 7 Nov 2026. Kya dekha: Repo ka mukhya page aur `README.md` ([https://github.com/joshyattridge/smart-money-concepts/blob/master/README.md](https://github.com/joshyattridge/smart-money-concepts/blob/master/README.md)) padha. Farak: README mein swing high/low ke liye aage ki candles bhi chahiye `[TAKRAAV #24]`. Dobara jaanch 9 Nov 2026: `smc.py` (987 lines) poori khol kar padhi. Usmein sirf numpy, pandas aur samay ke import hain, network, file ya chaabi ka koi call nahi dikha. Swing high/low pehchaanne mein aage ki candles bhi lagti hain, yaani pehle se likha farak sahi nikla.

Teesra repo: `vollib/py_vollib` ([https://github.com/vollib/py_vollib](https://github.com/vollib/py_vollib)). Kaam: Section 7 (Strike ke Delta aur option bhav se Implied Volatility nikalne ke liye).
