Zaroori file: `vollib/black_scholes/implied_volatility.py`, `vollib/black_scholes/greeks/analytical.py`, `vollib/black/implied_volatility.py` aur `vollib/black/greeks/analytical.py`. License: MIT License. Maalik: `vollib` / Gammon Capital LLC (organisation).

Is teesre repo ki aakhri update May 2026 (6 mahine pehle, release 1.0.7) hai. Kitne log use karte hain: 444 stars, 97 forks. Jaanch ki tarikh: 7 Nov 2026. Kya dekha: Repo ka mukhya page padha. Dobara jaanch 9 Nov 2026: chaaron zaroori files repo mein maujood hain. Unke import aur network, file ya system call ki talaash ki; sirf numpy aur repo ke apne helper mile. Har file ki har line nahi padhi. Repo ka aakhri commit 30 Apr 2026 ka hai aur `LICENSE` file mein MIT License hai. Doosra vikalp `marketcalls/opengreeks` ([https://github.com/marketcalls/opengreeks](https://github.com/marketcalls/opengreeks)) hai par uske abhi kam stars (27 stars) hain.

Chautha repo: `marketcalls/openalgo` ([https://github.com/marketcalls/openalgo](https://github.com/marketcalls/openalgo)). Kaam: Section 3, 5, 7, 10, 13 aur 16 (Broker login, Option Chain, GEX, Max Pain, Order Slicing, Dashboard, Paper Mode). Zaroori file: `app.py` ([https://github.com/marketcalls/openalgo/blob/main/app.py](https://github.com/marketcalls/openalgo/blob/main/app.py)), `broker/` (Upstox ke liye `broker/upstox/api/auth_api.py`, `order_api.py`, `funds.py`, `data.py`, `margin_api.py`, `rate_limiter.py`), `services/`, `sandbox/` aur `websocket_proxy/` (in teen folders ki exact files tay karna baaki). License: AGPL-3.0 License. Maalik: MarketCalls / Rajandran R (`marketcalls`, organisation).

Is chauthe repo ki aakhri update 4 Nov 2026 (3 din pehle) hai. Kitne log use karte hain: 3.0k stars, 1.4k forks. Jaanch ki tarikh: 7 Nov 2026. Kya dekha: mukhya page ([https://github.com/marketcalls/openalgo/blob/main/README.md](https://github.com/marketcalls/openalgo/blob/main/README.md)) aur userguide ([https://github.com/marketcalls/openalgo/blob/main/docs/userguide/09-api-key-management/README.md](https://github.com/marketcalls/openalgo/blob/main/docs/userguide/09-api-key-management/README.md)) padhe, Dobara jaanch 9 Nov 2026: repo ka aakhri commit 6 Nov 2026 ka hai. `broker/angel/` mein Angel One ki files hain, par ab yeh bot Upstox se judta hai. 9 Nov 2026 ko `broker/upstox/` ki files sparse tareeke se utaar kar dekhi: usmein login, order, funds, data, margin, `rate_limiter.py`, streaming aur master contract hain, yaani Upstox ka support hai. Unmein sirf `api.upstox.com`, `api-hft.upstox.com`, `assets.upstox.com` aur `openalgo.in` ke pate mile; IP ya MAC address ki talaash ya system call nahi dikha. Upstox ke order ki file mein Algo Name ka koi zikr nahi mila `[Sawal 112]`. `master_contract_db.py` mein "GIFT NIFTY" ka naam aur Upstox ke world feed ke do segment (`GLOBAL_INDEX`, `GLOBAL_INDICATOR`) mile `[Sawal 12]`. Har file ki har line nahi padhi. `sandbox/` mein Paper Mode jaise manager files hain. Har file ki har line nahi padhi, sirf talaash ki. Upstox ke apne pate ke alawa `api.openai.com`, `api.tavily.com` aur `api.perplexity.ai` ke pate AI agent wale hisse mein mile `[Sawal 110]`. Farak: iske dashboard mein strategy aur order lagane ka rasta bhi hai jabki idea ka Dashboard sirf Mode Chunav, Start Control, Stop Control aur suraksha Controls deta hai `[TAKRAAV #6]`.

Paanchva repo: `BennyThadikaran/NseIndiaApi` ([https://github.com/BennyThadikaran/NseIndiaApi](https://github.com/BennyThadikaran/NseIndiaApi)). Kaam: Section 3 (Subah ka dividend check aur shaam ka NSE data) aur Section 7 (Option Chain, Max Pain, PCR, VIX). Zaroori file: `src/nse/NSE.py`, `src/nse/transport.py`, `src/nse/cookie_store.py`, `src/nse/retry.py` aur `src/nse/_utils.py`. License: GPL-3.0 License. Maalik: Benny Thadikaran (`BennyThadikaran`, vyakti).

Is paanchve repo ki aakhri update October 2026 (pichle hafte, release v5.1.1) hai. Kitne log use karte hain: 161 stars, 44 forks. Jaanch ki tarikh: 7 Nov 2026. Kya dekha: Repo ka mukhya page padha. Dobara jaanch 9 Nov 2026: `src/nse/` ki files ki talaash ki. Sirf `nseindia.com` aur `nsearchives.nseindia.com` ke pate mile. Corporate actions, Option Chain, Max Pain aur VIX ke hisse maujood hain. System call ya chaabi ka koi call nahi dikha. Har line nahi padhi. Repo ka aakhri commit 30 Oct 2026 ka hai.

Chhatha repo: `ranaroussi/yfinance` ([https://github.com/ranaroussi/yfinance](https://github.com/ranaroussi/yfinance)). Kaam: Section 3 aur Section 7 (`Macro_Divergence_Check` ke liye USD/INR, crude oil, bond yield aur global sanket). Zaroori file: `yfinance/base.py` ([https://github.com/ranaroussi/yfinance/blob/main/yfinance/base.py](https://github.com/ranaroussi/yfinance/blob/main/yfinance/base.py)), `yfinance/scrapers/quote.py` ([https://github.com/ranaroussi/yfinance/blob/main/yfinance/scrapers/quote.py](https://github.com/ranaroussi/yfinance/blob/main/yfinance/scrapers/quote.py)) aur `yfinance/live.py`. License: Apache-2.0 License. Maalik: Ran Aroussi (`ranaroussi`, vyakti).

Is chhathe repo ki aakhri update July 2026 (4 mahine pehle) hai. Kitne log use karte hain: 25.9k stars, 3.5k forks. Jaanch ki tarikh: 8 Nov 2026. Kya dekha: `yfinance/base.py` aur `yfinance/scrapers/quote.py` khol kar padhe; unmein sirf public bhav mangwane ka kaam hai. Dobara jaanch 9 Nov 2026: `yfinance/live.py` (351 lines) khol kar padhi; woh `streamer.finance.yahoo.com` se live stream leti hai. System call ya chaabi ka koi call nahi dikha. Repo ka aakhri commit 7 Nov 2026 ka hai. Farak: bhav thoda peeche ho sakta hai aur bond yield va GIFT Nifty ka live tick pakka nahi hai `[TAKRAAV #7]`.

Saatva repo: `Supervisor/supervisor` ([https://github.com/Supervisor/supervisor](https://github.com/Supervisor/supervisor)). Kaam: Section 5 (Bot ke saath alag chalne wala Pehredaar jo bot ke rukne ya crash hone ki nigrani kare). Zaroori file: `supervisor/supervisord.py`, `supervisor/supervisorctl.py` ([https://github.com/Supervisor/supervisor/blob/main/supervisor/supervisorctl.py](https://github.com/Supervisor/supervisor/blob/main/supervisor/supervisorctl.py)) aur `LICENSES.txt` ([https://github.com/Supervisor/supervisor/blob/main/LICENSES.txt](https://github.com/Supervisor/supervisor/blob/main/LICENSES.txt)). License: Repoze Public License (BSD-style). Maalik: Supervisor organisation (`Supervisor`).

Is saatve repo ki aakhri update 21 Dec 2025 hai, aur ise 9.2k stars va 1.3k forks mile hain. Jaanch ki tarikh: 8 Nov 2026. Kya dekha: mukhya page aur `LICENSES.txt` khol kar padhe, Dobara jaanch 9 Nov 2026: `supervisor/supervisord.py` aur `supervisor/supervisorctl.py` ke import aur network ya system call ki talaash ki. Koi baahari internet call nahi mila. `supervisorctl.py` apne control ke liye password poochne ka kaam karti hai. Har line nahi padhi. Farak: yeh sirf process dekhta hai, broker par trade kaatne ke liye alag exit hissa chahiye `[TAKRAAV #8]`. Economic calendar ke liye bharosemand repo abhi nahi mila (repo dhoondhna baaki).

Aathva repo: `upstox/upstox-python` ([https://github.com/upstox/upstox-python](https://github.com/upstox/upstox-python)). Kaam: Section 3, 5, 7, 10 aur 13 (Upstox broker se login, order lagana, live data aur order ki khabar, Real Money Mode ke liye). Zaroori file: `upstox_client/` folder (`api/`, `api_client.py`, `configuration.py`, `rest.py` aur `feeder/`). License: MIT License (`LICENSE.txt` mein MIT License hai). Maalik: Upstox (`upstox`, organisation; Upstox ka official SDK).

Is aathve repo ki aakhri update 7 Sep 2026 (version 2.30.0) hai, aur ise lagbhag 115 se 123 stars va 86 se 87 forks mile hain. Jaanch ki tarikh: 9 Nov 2026. Kya dekha: repo clone karke `setup.py`, `LICENSE.txt`, `upstox_client/api_client.py`, `configuration.py`, `rest.py` aur `feeder/` ki files ki talaash ki. Bahari pate mein sirf Upstox ke apne pate (`api.upstox.com`, `api-hft.upstox.com`, `api-sandbox.upstox.com`, `account.upstox.com` aur websocket feeds) mile. Public IP, MAC address, system call ya chaabi bahar bhejne ka koi kaam nahi dikha `[Sawal 109]`. Har file ki har line nahi padhi. Doosra vikalp `batpool/upstox-totp` (MIT License) login automatic karne ke liye hai, par use liya nahi kyunki Upstox login ko poori tarah automatic karne ki salah nahi deta.

Nauva repo: `python-telegram-bot/python-telegram-bot` ([https://github.com/python-telegram-bot/python-telegram-bot](https://github.com/python-telegram-bot/python-telegram-bot)). Kaam: Section 14 (Telegram par Range Alert aur baaki alert bhejna). Zaroori file: `src/telegram/_bot.py`. License: dual license; `LICENSE.dual` mein likha hai ki GPL-3.0 ya LGPL-3.0 mein se koi ek chun sakte hain. Maalik: `python-telegram-bot` (organisation). Aakhri update ki tarikh: 1 Nov 2026 (repo ka aakhri commit).

Is nauve repo ko kareeb 28.7k stars aur 6k forks mile hain (search ke nateeje se). Jaanch ki tarikh: 9 Nov 2026. Kya dekha: `LICENSE.dual`, `LICENSE.lesser` aur `src/telegram/` ki files ki talaash ki; bahari pate mein sirf `api.telegram.org` mila. `src/telegram/_utils/de_json.py` mein type ke naam padhne ke liye ek `eval` hai, aur `src/telegram/__main__.py` version batane ke liye `git describe` chalata hai. Chaabi ya data bahar bhejne ka koi kaam nahi dikha. Har line nahi padhi. Popularity dobara nahi dekhi. Kaunsa license lena hai, yeh Sawal 60 mein hai.

Jaanch ki koshish 9 Nov 2026: pehle repo ki `ta/volatility.py` khol kar padhi. Is file mein 1011 lines hain. Kareeb 900 lines tak padhi gayin: unmein sirf numpy, pandas aur `ta.utils` ke import hain aur sirf bhav ka ganit hai. Network, file ya chaabi se jude koi call nahi dikhe. Dobara jaanch 9 Nov 2026: file ke aakhri hisse (`ulcer_index` ke baad) bhi padhe aur `ta/volume.py` bhi khol kar padhi. Dono mein koi network, file ya chaabi ka call nahi dikha. Economic calendar ke liye kuch scraper repos search mein dikhe (jaise `AtaCanYmc/forex-pytory`, `akopdev/economic-calendar`). Inke license, maalik, aakhri update aur popularity ki jaanch nahi hui, isliye koi repo liya nahi gaya. Jaanch mein `ta/volume.py` VWAP aur `smart-money-concepts` swing ka farak (`[TAKRAAV #24]`) mila. Baaki repos ki talaash ka nateeja unke apne block mein likha hai. Economic calendar ka repo abhi nahi mila.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` **Dobara jaanch 10 Nov 2026 (Claude Code).** Saare 9 repos dobara khole gaye. Har repo apne alag folder mein clone hua, code chalaya nahi gaya, sirf padha aur talaash ki. Poora hisaab `docs/research/R_b_Repos_Licence.md` mein hai. Har faisla Prastaavit hai. "Surakshit hai" kahin nahi likha; sirf jo dekha woh likha.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `bukosabino/ta`: License MIT. Aakhri commit 18 Mar 2026 ("Update README.md"); aakhri PyPI release 0.11.0 (2 Nov 2023). 5.3k stars, 1.2k forks. Koi published advisory nahi. Faisla: indicator khud banenge, aur repo ki `test/data/` wali CSV files sirf jawab milane ke liye (test vector) li jaayein. Wajah: VWAP rolling hai, din-bhar ka nahi `[TAKRAAV #24]`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `joshyattridge/smart-money-concepts`: License MIT. Aakhri commit 3 Apr 2026 (version 0.0.27). 2.2k stars, 907 forks. Swing pehchaanne mein aage ki candles dekhi jaati hain (`smartmoneyconcepts/smc.py`), isliye Backtest mein bhavishya ka data ghus jaayega. Faisla: sirf samajhne ke liye padho, khud banao.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `vollib/py_vollib`: License MIT. Master ka aakhri commit 30 Apr 2026; PyPI par 1.0.11 aur 1.0.12 hain par GitHub tag v1.0.10 tak hi hai. 446 stars, 97 forks. Faisla: IV aur greeks khud banao, aur repo ki `tests/test_data.json` se jawab milao. Wajah: publish hua code repo se mel khata hai ya nahi, pata nahi.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `marketcalls/openalgo`: License AGPL-3.0. Aakhri commit 6 Nov 2026. 3.0k stars, 1.4k forks. GitHub par advisory GHSA-4vvw-xw2w-38g8 (1 Nov 2026, mail connection mein TLS jaanch band). Iske Upstox live data wale code mein bhi TLS jaanch band mili. Faisla: use nahi, copy nahi; sirf padhne ka reference `[TAKRAAV #6]`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `BennyThadikaran/NseIndiaApi`: License GPL-3.0. Aakhri commit 30 Oct 2026 (v5.1.1). 162 stars, 46 forks. Yeh NSE website ka unofficial rasta hai. Faisla: use nahi; chhutti aur Option Chain broker ke official rasta se.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `ranaroussi/yfinance`: License Apache-2.0. Aakhri commit 7 Nov 2026; release 1.8.0. 25.9k stars, 3.5k forks. README ke hisaab se Yahoo ka data "personal use only" hai. Faisla: bot ke andar nahi; sirf alag research jagah mein, bina broker chaabi ke `[TAKRAAV #7]`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `Supervisor/supervisor`: License BSD-derived (Repoze). Aakhri commit 21 Dec 2025; release 4.3.0. 9.2k stars, 1.3k forks. Faisla: zaroorat nahi; machine ke apne process-nigrani wale tareeke (restart aur watchdog) kaafi hain. Broker par trade kaatne ke liye Pehredaar ka Exit Hissa phir bhi alag hai `[TAKRAAV #8]`.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `upstox/upstox-python`: License MIT (Upstox ka official SDK). Aakhri commit 9 Nov 2026; release 2.30.0. 193 stars, 128 forks. Koi published advisory nahi. `[F-64 | High]` Iske live data wale hisse (`upstox_client/feeder/`) mein TLS certificate jaanch band mili, aur usi connection par token jaata hai. Faisla: order aur account wala hissa pinned aur hash-locked le sakte hain; live data ka hissa khud banao, TLS jaanch chalu rakh kar, broker ki official feed definition file pin karke (Sawal 109).

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `python-telegram-bot/python-telegram-bot`: License GPL-3.0 ya LGPL-3.0 (malik chune). Aakhri commit 9 Nov 2026; release 22.8. 29.6k stars, 6.2k forks. Faisla: library ki zaroorat nahi; alert sirf Telegram ke official Bot API ([https://core.telegram.org/bots/api](https://core.telegram.org/bots/api)) se seedha bheja jaaye (Sawal 60 ka prastaavit jawab).

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Naye vikalp (10 Nov 2026 ko dekhe): Upstox ke official SDK Python, Node.js, Java, PHP aur .NET mein hain; Rust, Go aur C++ ke liye koi official SDK nahi mila. Live data ki official definition file: [https://assets.upstox.com/feed/market-data-feed/v3/MarketDataFeed.proto](https://assets.upstox.com/feed/market-data-feed/v3/MarketDataFeed.proto). Indicator ke liye `TA-Lib/ta-lib` (BSD, aakhri commit 9 Nov 2026) doosra vikalp hai. `twopirllc/pandas-ta` 10 Nov 2026 ko khula hi nahi, isliye nahi lena. Economic calendar ke liye koi bharosemand repo nahi mila; official srot: RBI MPC schedule ([https://www.rbi.org.in/scripts/BS_PressReleaseDisplay.aspx?prid=62422](https://www.rbi.org.in/scripts/BS_PressReleaseDisplay.aspx?prid=62422)) aur MoSPI Advance Release Calendar. Strike-wise GEX ka koi official srot nahi mila (Sawal 92 ab bhi khula).

## 19. Abhi tay nahi hua

Is section ke teen hisse hain. Pehle hisse mein woh sawal hain jinka prastaavit jawab file mein likh diya gaya hai; malik ko sirf haan ya nahi kehna hai. Doosre hisse mein woh sawal hain jo malik ya asli data par tike hain. Teesre hisse mein takraav hain. Jawab milte hi sawal yahan se hat jaata hai aur jawab sahi ghar mein likha jaata hai.

### [PRASTAAVIT-CC] Naye sawal (10 Nov 2026), sabse zaroori upar

Yeh sawal 10 Nov 2026 ke audit se aaye hain (dekho: `01b_Audit_Findings.md`). Purane sawal dobara nahi likhe; jahan purana sawal ab zyada zaroori hai, wahan sirf uska number likha hai.

- Sawal 117 (Pehredaar ka haq): kya Pehredaar ka alag Upstox app bot ke app ke order cancel aur position band kar sakta hai? Kyun zaroori: poora Pehredaar isi par tika hai; Real Money Mode se pehle asli test chahiye `[F-06]`.
- Sawal 118 (Lot ka niyam): poora balance wala niyam rakhna hai ya Vikalp A, B ya C? Kyun zaroori: abhi ek Stop-Loss se din, hafte aur mahine ki teeno seemayein toot-ti hain `[F-03]`.
- Sawal 119 (NA ke do roop): NA-Data aur NA-Lagu-Nahi manzoor? Kyun zaroori: iske bina aam din par manipulation Gates har trade rokte hain `[F-01]`.
- Sawal 16 ab Critical hai: Nifty50 aur Sensex ki stocks suchi ke bina Index_Breadth_Check Gate un index ko band rakhta hai. Jawab tak `Breadth_Top_Stocks_Ginti` wala prastaav aur sirf Paper Mode `[F-02]`.
- Sawal 120 (Kill Latch): Kill-Switch ke baad us din bot band, aur agle din Start Control se hi khule? Kyun zaroori: abhi Kill-Switch ke baad bot phir entry le sakta hai `[F-04]`.
- Sawal 121 (Lease): Pehredaar ke control lene par us din bot broker ko koi hukm na bheje? Kyun zaroori: dono ek saath chale toh net short ho sakta hai `[F-05]`.
- Sawal 122 (Real Money ka taala): Backtest Gate pass hone tak Real Money Mode band, aur Stage Override sirf `Stage_Override_Max_Lot`? Kyun zaroori: abhi parakh ek click se chhoot sakti hai `[F-18]`.
- Sawal 123 (Stage ke paimane): `Backtest_Min_Avadhi`, `Paper_Gate_CC`, `Stage2_Gate_CC` aur `Live_Kill_Paimana` manzoor? Kyun zaroori: abhi ke paimane kismat aur hunar ko alag nahi karte `[F-19]`.
- Sawal 124 (stop ka chunav): ATR aur swing low mein se door wala stop, aur cap se door ho toh trade skip (H-06) ya cap par stop? Kyun zaroori: yeh har trade ka nuksan tay karta hai `[F-22]`.
- Sawal 125 (stop ka bhav): bot ka Stop-Loss best bid par `Stop_Pushti_Ginti` ke saath chale? Kyun zaroori: galat bhav par stop chalna seedha nuksan hai `[F-21]`.
- Sawal 126 (haath ka trade): Foreign Position wale instrument mein bot trade na kare? Kyun zaroori: warna bot malik ke Lot bech sakta hai `[F-11]`.
- Sawal 127 (din ki seema): naye entry se pehle khule trade ka Worst-Case Nuksan bhi din ki seema mein gina jaaye? Kyun zaroori: warna do trade milkar seema tod dete hain `[F-14]`.
- Sawal 128 (feed atakna): `Blind_Position_Hadd` par pehle broker se bhav maang kar chalna (H-15), ya turant MPP exit? Kyun zaroori: chhote atkaav par mehenga exit bachta hai `[F-33]`.
- Sawal 129 (aapat rasta): Dashboard band ho toh Kill-Switch ka doosra rasta kya ho? Kyun zaroori: abhi sirf Upstox app bachta hai `[F-47]`.
- Sawal 130 (range ka kaam): `Target_Premium_Range` sirf paise ki seema hai ya malik ki premium pasand bhi? Kyun zaroori: isi se Premium_Range_Gate aur Range Alert ka matlab tay hota hai `[F-24]`.
- Sawal 131 (expiry subah): expiry ki subah Kamzor Breakout par koi trade nahi, ya agli expiry? Kyun zaroori: abhi niyam chup hai `[F-30]`.
- Sawal 132 (Profit Lock): dopahar ki kamai par bhi Daily Profit Lock lage? Kyun zaroori: niyam mein sirf "subah ke trades" likha hai `[F-50]`.
- Sawal 133 (range wale Pakka numbers): `Aas_Paas_Strikes_Ginti`, `Chart_Range_Breakout_Samay`, `Mazboot_Breakout_Tikav_Samay`, `Kamzor_Breakout_Tikav_Samay`, `Daily_Max_Auto_Entries` aur `Premium_Max_Stop_Loss_Percent` mein se har ek ki ek hi kimat kya ho? Kyun zaroori: code ek hi number par chalta hai `[F-55]`.
- Sawal 134 (BankNifty ki suchi): BankNifty ke banks ki ginti index ki official suchi se roz li jaaye? Kyun zaroori: exchange ginti badal sakta hai `[F-58]`.
- Sawal 135 (data kharid): Backtest ke liye kaunsa purana data aur kitne budget mein kharidna hai (dekho: `03_Strategy_Edge_Plan.md`)? Kyun zaroori: data ki quality niyam se zyada nateeja badalti hai.
- Sawal 136 (Pehredaar aur ek API key): kya Pehredaar ka alag Upstox app NSE ke "ek pehle se tay API key" wale niyam ke andar allowed hai? Kyun zaroori: na ho toh Pehredaar ka design badalna padega; Upstox aur vakil se likhit jawab chahiye `[F-62]`.
- Sawal 137 (naye bazaar samay): 3:40 PM aur 7 Dec 2026 ke baad 3:45 PM (expiry din 3:30 PM) ke hisaab se Gamma Blast Zone ka ant, `Aakhri_Entry_Samay` aur `Auto_Square_Off_Samay` kya ho? Kyun zaroori: samay ke niyam purane bazaar par bane hain `[F-63]`.
- Sawal 138 (broker ka Kill Switch): Kill-Switch ke baad broker ka segment Kill Switch bhi chale? Kyun zaroori: bot ki galti ho tab bhi naya order ruke, par malik ka manual trade bhi rukega `[F-66]`.
- Sawal 139 (login ka tareeka): Upstox ka official TOTP login rasta aa gaya hai. Kya roz ka login TOTP se ho (secret machine par rakhna padega) ya phone manzoori se? Kyun zaroori: Sawal 113 ka faisla ab do asli raston mein hai; vakil se bhi poochna hai.
- Sawal 140 (data ka licence): NSE ke purane data ki licence mein "commercial use" ki pabandi ki line hai. Kya apne paise ke bot ke Backtest ke liye allowed hai? Kyun zaroori: kharid se pehle NSE se likhit jawab chahiye.
- Sawal 141 (shared token): kya Upstox ka ek hi token Primary aur Secondary Static IP se ek saath (bot aur Pehredaar) chal sakta hai, aur kya Pehredaar is token se bot ke order cancel aur position band kar sakta hai? Kyun zaroori: broker ke "ek user, ek app" niyam ke baad Pehredaar ka yahi ek rasta bachta hai; Real Money se pehle asli test `[F-76]` `[F-06]`.

### Malik ki pushti ki suchi (prastaavit jawab, haan ya nahi)

**Broker, login aur hosting**

- Sawal 2 (broker): broker Upstox hai (user ne kaha). Upstox ya data band ho toh naye entry band, Broker Stop khula, Pehredaar dekhta hai (dekho: Pehredaar aur Suraksha).
- Sawal 15 (chaabi aur login): chaabi sirf surakshit store mein; roz subah malik phone par login manzoor karta hai (dekho: Bot ka ek poora din).
- Sawal 75 (Static IP, OPS, product): order sirf Primary Static IP se, Algo Name ke saath, Intraday (MIS) product par; reject par alag kaam (dekho: Pehredaar aur Suraksha).
- Sawal 90 (Pehredaar ka login): Upstox mein ek app ka ek hi token chalta hai, isliye Pehredaar ka alag app aur Secondary Static IP hai.
- Sawal 14 (ghadi): subah `Ghadi_Skew_Hadd` ke andar ghadi ka milaan, warna Taiyari Fail.
- Sawal 13 (exchange ke badlaav): Lot, freeze limit aur expiry roz Instrument Master se.
- Sawal 11 (IV record): jab tak apna `IVP_Lookback_Din` ka record na bane, India VIX ka percentile.

**Paisa, Lot aur Fund Request**

- Sawal 7 (kam se kam balance): 1 Lot ka kharcha aur `Balance_Extra_Buffer`; usse kam par Start Control band.
- Sawal 49 (premium ka matlab): premium ek unit ka bhav hai; 1 Lot ka kharcha premium ko `Ek_Lot_Quantity` se guna karke.
- Sawal 50 (range ke kinaare): `Entry_Order_Slippage_Buffer` range ke andar ginta hai.
- Sawal 57 (extra paisa): 1 Lot ke kharche ka `Balance_Extra_Buffer`.
- Sawal 61 (range ka aadhar): Delta band ke strikes ke bhav se, free paisa ki seema se kati hui (`Range_Tay_Karne_Ka_Tarika`).
- Sawal 62 (fund der se aaya): nayi Fund Request nahi, jo paisa hai usmein sabse achha premium.
- Sawal 63 (dopahar mein extra fund): dopahar mein doosri Fund Request nahi.
- Sawal 64 (Lot aur Fund Request): Lot asli free paisa se.
- Sawal 65 (Start se pehle paisa): free paisa kam ho toh Start Control band aur \"Paisa Kam\".
- Sawal 69 (range 9:15 ke baad): haan, teeno index ke liye alag, 9:15 ke baad ke asli bhav se.
- Sawal 59 (average down): nahi, khule trade mein Lot nahi judta.
- Sawal 35 (Lot ke adhoore niyam): mazboot signal par balance ki seema tak; kamzor par ek Lot kam; Lot `Lot_Wapasi_Paimana` se wapas.
- Sawal 28 (dono Lot cut ek saath): Lot sirf ek baar aadhe.

**Risk Limit**

- Sawal 5 (poora bot band): mahine ka nuksan `Monthly_Loss_Risk_Limit` paar kare toh poora bot; malik report dekh kar Start dabaye.
- Sawal 58 (ek trade ka nuksan): `Premium_Max_Stop_Loss_Percent` se bandha; alag rupaye ki hadd nahi.
- Sawal 66 (din aur hafte ki Risk Limit): `Daily_Loss_Risk_Limit`, `Weekly_Loss_Risk_Limit` aur Daily Profit Lock rehte hain.
- Sawal 67 (1 Lot par percent rules): Risk Limit trade band hone ke baad ginti hai; 1 Lot par Lot cut nahi lagta. Is se ek Stop-Loss ke baad din band ho sakta hai.
- Sawal 68 (balance ki paribhasha): Deposit-Adjusted Equity.
- Sawal 95 (taak Lot aur Profit Lock): taak Lot ho toh aadhe se kam bechna; Profit Lock ke baad bache munafe ke andar wala trade.

**Score, Gate aur checks**

- Sawal 9 (pehle version ke checks): Check Suchi ka \"Pehle version mein?\" column; \"baad mein\" wale Shadow Mode mein.
- Sawal 19 (RED ki shart aur vazan): har check ke block aur Check Suchi mein.
- Sawal 33 (group cap): us waqt ke zaroori score ka `Ek_Group_Maximum_Score_Hissa`.
- Sawal 37 (GEX check ka Group): Group Directional-Bias, Asar sirf Confidence.
- Sawal 36 (IVP medium aur Delta): `Delta_Filter` mein `Expiry_Subah_Delta_Range` jaise andar wala hissa.
- Sawal 32 (strike ka chunav): jiska score bar se sabse upar, phir kam Slippage, phir kam Lot kharcha (dekho: Faisle ka raasta).
- Sawal 70 (checks ki gati aur NA): saare checks `Evaluation_Gati_Samay` par; data na ho toh NA.
- Sawal 71 (beech ki sthiti): GREEN aur RED dono sach na ho toh Gate ke liye RED, Score ke liye 0.
- Sawal 91 (Iceberg aur feed ki gati): data na mile toh NA; dono checks Shadow Mode mein.
- Sawal 93 (Option Chain ka samay): ek hi snapshot, `Option_Chain_Snapshot_Samay_Farak` ke andar.
- Sawal 98 (kharcha ka hisaab): Delta aur Theta se munafa, broker ki charge suchi se kharcha.
- Sawal 99 (IV ka tareeka): bid aur ask ke beech ke bhav se, teeno checks mein ek hi tareeka.
- Sawal 100 (Premium tezi aur Chase Guard): apni 5 minute ki ausat raftaar; breakout candle ke band hone par naap.
- Sawal 101 (Disha): `Disha_Paribhasha`.
- Sawal 102 (Max Pain): sirf `Max_Pain_Paas_Hadd` mein.
- Sawal 103 (indicator ka hisaab): repo ka hisaab; Paper Stage mein broker chart se ek baar milaan.
- Sawal 78 aur 79 (VIX, PCR, Macro ki disha aur chart parameters): har check ke block aur Numbers ki list mein.
- Sawal 108 (ruka hua stock): `Ruka_Stock_Hadd`.
- Sawal 30, 31 aur 72 (Din ka Mausam): `Ulta_Palat_Pehchaan_Paimana`, `ADX_Trending_Hadd`, `ADX_Sideways_Hadd`; candles apne tick se, 9:15 se.
- Sawal 38 va 39 (avadhi aur khaali hadd): Numbers ki list ke prastaavit maan.
- Sawal 18 (vague shabdon ke numbers): Numbers ki list ke prastaavit maan.

**Order, Broker Stop aur Pehredaar**

- Sawal 73 va 87 (exit aur Kill-Switch ka kram): Broker Stop cancel, confirm, phir exit; exit fill na ho toh Broker Stop dobara.
- Sawal 74 (atke bot mein Kill-Switch): hukm seedha Pehredaar ke Exit Hissa ko.
- Sawal 82 (entry ke baad Broker Stop): `Entry_Ke_Baad_Broker_Stop_Samay` mein, warna trade band.
- Sawal 83 (bot dobara chalu): pehle positions padhna; bina Broker Stop wale trade ko pehle Broker Stop.
- Sawal 84 (aadha fill): Broker Stop ki quantity fill ke barabar; 1 Lot se kam fill turant band.
- Sawal 85 (cancel ka samay): broker ke manjoor karne se; fill ho gaya toh fill.
- Sawal 86 (ek hi signal): Signal ID aur `Signal_Dobara_Order_Roak_Samay`.
- Sawal 88 (Broker Stop badalna fail): do baar reject par trade band.
- Sawal 89 (request ki seema): Order Baari ka kram.
- Sawal 94 (bhav ka chhota kadam): Tick Size aur Circuit-limit ke andar.
- Sawal 96 (square-off): ek samay, broker ke square-off se pehle.
- Sawal 80 (ulte checks se exit): `Ulta_Check_Tikav_Samay`; do Stop-Loss mein jo pehle chale.
- Sawal 81 (haath ka trade aur alert backup): Foreign Position ko bot nahi chhoota; critical alert email se bhi.
- Sawal 97 (Cooldown): ek kimat `Loss_Cooldown_Samay`; naya breakout ki pehchaan Entry mein.
- Sawal 77 (BankNifty monthly): shift sirf monthly expiry din; 1 Lot ka order tukdon mein nahi.
- Sawal 34 (Stop-Loss ke numbers): Numbers ki list ke ATR aur Trailing numbers.

**Dashboard, Mode aur alert**

- Sawal 21 (Dashboard ki pahunch): sirf malik, login ke baad doosra step.
- Sawal 22 (Kill-Switch ki suraksha): Kill-Switch, Stop Control aur Real Money Start par confirm step.
- Sawal 23 va 104 (refresh aur sachchai): `Dashboard_Refresh_Samay`; har dabbe ke saath data ki umar.
- Sawal 42 (Stop Control ka asar): naye entry band, khule trade apne exit se band hon, phir bot ruke.
- Sawal 43 (Mode badalna): chalte bot mein nahi; dono mode ek saath nahi.
- Sawal 44 (Paper ka balance aur record): `Paper_Mode_Balance`; record alag.
- Sawal 45 (Real Money ka Lot): Stage ke hisaab se; Stage Override alag.
- Sawal 46 (Stop Control aur Pehredaar): \"Malik ne roka\" nishan.
- Sawal 48 (Start par taiyari kharab): \"Taiyari Fail\" aur `Taiyari_Dobara_Koshish_Ginti`.
- Sawal 51 (tagada ki pehchaan): wahi Gates aur Scorecard.
- Sawal 52 (range ke bahar ki upari hadd): `Range_Alert_Upari_Premium`.
- Sawal 53 (alert ke baad): malik Upstox app se khud khareede; website par button nahi.
- Sawal 54 va 55 (alert ki ginti aur aakhri samay): `Alert_Cooldown_Samay`, `Daily_Max_Alerts`, `Alert_Aakhri_Samay`.
- Sawal 56 (Telegram fail): dobara bhejna, phir email aur Dashboard.
- Sawal 25 (alert par malik): alert ke darje ke hisaab se (dekho: Alerts aur insaan ki bhoomika).
- Sawal 107 (khaas din ki suchi): malik ki calendar file; suchi mein na mile din par bot trade nahi leta.

**Report, Record aur Test**

- Sawal 26 (niyam badalne ki jagah): alag niyam-file; har badlaav ka record.
- Sawal 27 (record ki avadhi): `Record_Rakhne_Avadhi`; tax ke liye roz alag file.
- Sawal 105 (reject signal ka nateeja): bid-ask ke beech ke bhav se, `Reject_Signal_Nateeja_Samay` par ek baar.
- Sawal 4 va 6 (kamyabi aur stage ka paimana): `Stage_Badlaav_Paimana`, `Paper_Mode_Avadhi`, `Stage2_Avadhi`.
- Sawal 8 (Backtest): haan, Paper Mode se pehle.
- Sawal 10 (trades ki aam ginti): `Daily_Max_Auto_Entries` ke andar; `Trade_Kam_Din_Ginti` se jaanch.
- Sawal 106 (test ke trades aur badlaav): `Paper_Mode_Trade_Ginti`, `Paper_Asli_Fill_Farak_Hadd`; badlaav par Stage dobara shuru nahi.

### Sawal: ab bhi khule (malik ya asli data par tike)

1. Kiske liye: bot sirf apne account ke liye hai ya baad mein doosron ke liye bhi? Kyun zaroori: regulator ke algo niyam dono mein alag lagte hain; Upstox ka algo-vendor rasta alag hai.
3. Bot, Pehredaar aur website kahan chalenge: bot aur Pehredaar alag machine par (Primary aur Secondary Static IP) prastaavit hain, par machine kahan hogi aur website kahan se khulegi? Kyun zaroori: net ya bijli jaane par khula trade tabhi bachega jab Pehredaar alag jagah ho.
12. Macro data ka srot: USD/INR, crude oil aur bond yield Upstox ke instruments mein milte hain ya nahi, aur GIFT Nifty ki live gati kaisi hai? Kyun zaroori: `Macro_Divergence_Check` ka data pakka nahi hai.
16. Nifty50 aur Sensex ke stocks: Nifty50 aur Sensex ke Breadth, manipulation check aur Sensex ke Heavyweight Stocks ke naam kaunse hain? Kyun zaroori: abhi sirf BankNifty ke 12 banks aur Nifty50 ke 4 stocks likhe hain.
17. Sensex ki Freeze Limit aur pakke liquidity numbers: Sensex ki freeze limit nahi mili, aur Sensex ka liquidity number prastaavit hai. Kyun zaroori: Order Slicing aur Liquidity_Filter ke liye.
20. Repo ki poori jaanch aur economic calendar repo: jin repos ki har line nahi padhi gayi unki poori jaanch kab hogi, aur economic calendar ke liye kaunsa repo ya srot? Kyun zaroori: asli paise aur broker ki chaabi ke saath bina poori file jaanche koi repo nahi lagana chahiye.
29. Andaze wale numbers ki pushti: Numbers ki list ke jin numbers ka status \"Andaza\" ya \"Prastaavit\" hai, unmein se kaunse pakke hain? Kyun zaroori: final PRD mein pakke numbers chahiye.
40. Repo ka license: `marketcalls/openalgo` AGPL-3.0 aur `BennyThadikaran/NseIndiaApi` GPL-3.0 par hain. Kya yeh Sawal 1 ke jawab par asar daalega? Kyun zaroori: license ka asar tab padta hai jab bot doosron ke liye ho.
41. `bukosabino/ta` ki umar: uski `ta/` files 3 saal purani hain. Kya yeh repo rakhna hai? Kyun zaroori: niyam aur repo ki sthiti takra rahi hain.
60. Telegram ka repo: license saaf hai (GPL-3.0 ya LGPL-3.0). Kya yeh repo lena hai, aur kaunsa license? Kyun zaroori: license ka asar Sawal 1 par padta hai.
76. Upstox ka feed: ek connection par kitne instrument aur kitne connection milenge, aur rollover par kaunsa Futures lenge? Kyun zaroori: har strike par har second tick nahi aata aur subscription seema bhi hai.
92. GEX ka data: strike-wise dealers ka gamma kahan se aayega? Kyun zaroori: `GEX_Positioning_Check` ka srot nahi mila.
109. Upstox SDK ki poori jaanch: 9 Nov 2026 ki talaash mein sirf Upstox ke apne pate mile aur IP ya chaabi bhejne ka kaam nahi dikha, par har file ki har line kab padhi jayegi? Kyun zaroori: asli paise aur broker ki chaabi ke saath bina poori file padhe SDK nahi lagana chahiye.
110. openalgo ka AI agent: uska AI agent wala hissa bahari AI aur search sevaon ko call karta hai. Kya repo ka sirf broker, order aur data wala hissa liya jayega? Kyun zaroori: broker ki chaabi wale bot se data bahar jaane ka rasta nahi hona chahiye.
111. Order ke raste ki jaanch: Upstox par bina asli order lagaye Static IP aur login ki jaanch ka koi rasta hai ya nahi? Kyun zaroori: `Order_Path_Jaanch_Samay` ki jaanch isi par tiki hai.
112. Algo-ID aur registration: Upstox ke hisaab se 10 order prati second se kam par registration nahi chahiye. Kya is par Algo-ID bhi chahiye? Kyun zaroori: bot ke order par kaunsi pehchaan lagegi.
113. Roz ke login ka tareeka: phone par manzoori (semi-automatic) ya TOTP wala rasta? Kyun zaroori: Upstox login ko poori tarah automatic karne ki salah nahi deta, aur subah Start Control iske baad hi chalta hai.
114. Upstox ka apna square-off: Upstox apne aap kab Intraday positions band karta hai? Kyun zaroori: `Auto_Square_Off_Samay` ko usse pehle rakhna hai.
115. Delta aur IV ka srot: Upstox ka option greeks wala data lena hai ya IV khud nikalna hai? Kyun zaroori: Delta_Filter aur teen IV checks ek hi srot par chalne chahiye.
116. Upstox ka sandbox: Paper Mode ke liye Upstox ka sandbox lena hai ya bot ka apna Paper Mode? Kyun zaroori: Paper Mode ko asli Market Depth chahiye.

### Takraav (do jagah ki baat takraati hai)

Har takraav ke saath `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` ek line mein likha hai. Jab tak malik pushti na de, takraav khula maana jaata hai.

- **[TAKRAAV #1] Confirmation banam Khaas Din ki Manzoori:** Daayra aur Entry mein likha hai ki bot \"entry ke liye kisi se confirmation nahi maangta\", par subah ki taiyari kehti hai ki khaas din par bot \"bina malik ki manzoori ke\" trade nahi leta. Nayi baat: Start dabane par hi bot active hota hai. `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` Start ke baad har entry bot khud karta hai; khaas din par bot trade nahi leta aur manzoori ka button nahi hai.
- **[TAKRAAV #2] Momentum Zone ka ant (11:45 AM) banam Manipulation Check 1 ka samay (11:47 AM):** Momentum Zone `11:45 AM` tak chalta hai, par `Manipulation_Check1_Samay` ka subah ka samay `11:47 AM` tak likha hai. `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` dono ka ant `11:45 AM` rakha jaaye.
- **[TAKRAAV #3] Shuruaati dekhne ka samay (Pakka banam Andaza):** Ek jagah `15 se 20 minute`, doosri jagah `kuch minute (jaise 15 se 20 minute)` likha tha. `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` 15 minute, `Shuruaati_Dekhne_Ka_Samay`.
- **[TAKRAAV #4] Lagatar 2 nuksan par din band banam Lagatar 3 ulte auto-entries par confidence sakht:** Ek jagah 2 nuksan par din band hota hai, doosri jagah 3 lagatar ulte entry par confidence sakht hota hai. `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` dono ginti alag hain; ulte entry ki ginti din badalne par bhi chalti hai (dekho: Entry).
- **[TAKRAAV #5] Gamma Blast Zone ka naam banam Expiry dopahar ka shift:** Gamma Blast Zone tez momentum ka zone hai, par expiry wale index mein `1:30 PM` ke baad agli expiry ka strike aata hai. `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` zone ka naam Gamma Blast Zone hi rahega aur shift ka niyam Expiry ke din ke niyam mein hai.
- **[TAKRAAV #6] OpenAlgo repo ka Dashboard banam Idea ka Read-Only Dashboard:** `marketcalls/openalgo` ke dashboard mein strategy aur order lagane ki suvidha hai, jabki idea ka Dashboard sirf Mode Chunav, Start Control, Stop Control aur suraksha Controls deta hai. `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` repo ka sirf broker, order, data aur Paper Mode wala hissa liya jaaye; uska dashboard nahi.
- **[TAKRAAV #7] Macro Divergence ka Second-Level Live Bhav banam `ranaroussi/yfinance`:** Idea ko macro sanket kuch second pehle chahiye, par `yfinance` mein bhav peeche ho sakta hai aur GIFT Nifty ka live tick pakka nahi. `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` GIFT Nifty Upstox ki Global Instruments file se; baaki macro sanket Upstox mein na milein toh `yfinance` sirf jaankari ke liye (Sawal 12).
- **[TAKRAAV #8] Pehredaar ka Trade Band Karna banam `Supervisor/supervisor`:** `Supervisor/supervisor` sirf process dekhta hai, broker par trade nahi kaat-ta. `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` Pehredaar ka Exit Hissa alag chhota hissa hai jo broker par trade kaatta hai.
- **[TAKRAAV #9] Khule trade ka nikalna banam Stop-Loss tight karna:** Section 3 aur 4 mein \"nikaalta hai ya Stop-Loss tight karta hai\" tha, Section 11 mein \"nikaale jaate hain\". `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` har sthiti mein trade band hota hai (dekho: Exit).
- **[TAKRAAV #10] Confidence ki badhotri banam Maximum Score Cap:** Ek baar 10 se 15 points ki badhotri aur cap 25 hone se cap kabhi lagta hi nahi tha. `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` har kaaran par `Confidence_Badhotri_Points` aur `Maximum_Score_Threshold_Cap` alag.
- **[TAKRAAV #11] Roz subah apne aap chalu banam website se Start:** Bot ka process roz subah chalu hota hai, par trade tabhi jab malik Start Control dabaye. `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` process roz apne aap chalu, Start malik ka (dekho: Bot ka ek poora din).
- **[TAKRAAV #12] Test ke teen charan banam malik ka koi bhi mode chunna:** `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` malik koi bhi mode chun sakta hai; Paper Stage se pehle Real Money Mode Stage2 ke Lot par chalta hai aur \"Stage Override\" dikhta hai.
- **[TAKRAAV #13] Balance ke hisaab se Lot banam Lot badhane-ghatane ke purane niyam:** `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` balance upari seema hai; mazboot signal par seema tak, kamzor par ek Lot kam.
- **[TAKRAAV #15] Dusra Lot banam ek index mein ek khula trade:** `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` saare Lot ek order aur ek trade mein; doosra alag trade nahi.
- **[TAKRAAV #16] Start se pehle bot dekhta hai banam Start dabane par hi active:** `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` Start se pehle bot sirf dekhta hai aur Fund Request bhejta hai; trade sirf Start ke baad.
- **[TAKRAAV #17] Poore balance se 1 Lot aur 25-30% Stop-Loss banam 3% Daily Risk Limit aur 50% Lot Cut:** `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` Risk Limit trade band hone ke baad ginti hai aur 1 Lot par Lot cut nahi lagta. Phir bhi ek Stop-Loss se din band ho sakta hai, isliye malik ko `Daily_Loss_Risk_Limit` ki kimat par dobara vichar karna hai.
- **[TAKRAAV #18] Ek hi Target Premium Range banam teeno index ke Lot kharche, Delta range aur 1:30 PM shift:** `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` range har index ke liye alag aur shift par dobara tay; koi strike na aaye toh us index mein trade nahi.
- **[TAKRAAV #19] Faisle ka raasta mein aage ke kadam rukna (§6) banam Dashboard ke live dabbe (§13):** `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` saare checks hamesha chalte hain aur sirf order rukta hai.
- **[TAKRAAV #20] Scorecard mein tay vazan ka jod banam Confidence Badhotri:** `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` har check ka vazan Check Suchi mein likha hai aur kul jod `Check_Vazan_Kul_Jod` hai.
- **[TAKRAAV #21] Lambi Wick Check banam Chase Guard, aur Pre-Breakout Alert banam Fake Spike Filter:** `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` Lambi_Wick_Check aur Chase_Guard dono breakout candle ke band hone par naape jaate hain; Pre_Breakout_Alert_Check Shadow Mode mein hai.
- **[TAKRAAV #22] Target Premium Range (200 se 300) banam Range Alert Upari Premium (kareeb 300):** `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` `Range_Alert_Upari_Premium` range ke upari kinare se 20 percent upar hai.
- **[TAKRAAV #23] Expiry din 1:30 PM ke baad agli expiry mein trade (§4) banam Manipulation Settlement Check (§7):** `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` SUSPICIOUS flag us index mein agli expiry ke trade ko bhi rokta hai.
- **[TAKRAAV #24] Repo `bukosabino/ta` ka rolling VWAP aur `smart-money-concepts` ka swing lookahead banam Idea ke niyam (§17):** `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` din-bhar ka VWAP aur confirmed candles ka alag hisaab lagega, repo ka window wala VWAP nahi.
- **[TAKRAAV #25] Zone ka lagbhag 1:30 PM (Andaza) banam `Expiry_Shift_Aur_Exit_Samay` 1:30 PM (Pakka):** `[PRASTAAVIT HAL]` zone ki seema aur expiry ka shift ek hi pakka samay, 1:30 PM.
- **[TAKRAAV #26] `[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Gate ka NA RED banam sirf expiry din wale manipulation Gates:** Aam din par Manipulation_Cash_Volume_Check aur Manipulation_Settlement_Check lagu nahi, isliye NA aur RED hokar har trade rokte hain. `[PRASTAAVIT-CC HAL]` NA ke do roop: NA-Data (Gate RED) aur NA-Lagu-Nahi (Gate par asar nahi) `[F-01]`.
- **[TAKRAAV #27] `[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `Ghadi_Skew_Hadd` banam `Data_Purana_Hadd`:** Ghadi ki chhoot data ki taazgi ki seema jitni badi hai. `[PRASTAAVIT-CC HAL]` `Ghadi_Skew_Sakht_Hadd` aur data ki umar apne paane ke samay se bhi `[F-07]`.
- **[TAKRAAV #28] `[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Poore balance ka Lot banam hafte aur mahine ki Risk Limit:** Ek Stop-Loss `Monthly_Loss_Risk_Limit` se bhi bada ho sakta hai, isliye pehle nuksan par bot mahine bhar rukta hai. `[PRASTAAVIT-CC HAL]` malik Vikalp A, B ya C chune; tab tak Monte Carlo ka nateeja malik ko dikhe `[F-03]`.
- **[TAKRAAV #29] `[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Kill-Switch "sab kuch rokta hai" banam hukm sirf Pehredaar ko:** Bot ko Kill-Switch ka pata nahi chalta. `[PRASTAAVIT-CC HAL]` Kill Latch jise bot aur Pehredaar dono padhte hain `[F-04]`.
- **[TAKRAAV #30] `[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `Trailing_Stop_Doori` banam `ATR_Stop_Guna`:** Trailing ki doori pehle stop se chhoti hai aur shuru hone ka samay likha nahi. `[PRASTAAVIT-CC HAL]` trailing breakeven ke baad shuru `[F-20]`.
- **[TAKRAAV #31] `[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Har khiskaav par Broker Stop badalna banam broker ki din ki modify seema:** `[PRASTAAVIT-CC HAL]` `Broker_Stop_Min_Khiskaav` aur `Broker_Stop_Modify_Bajat` `[F-08]`.
- **[TAKRAAV #32] `[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Index_Breadth_Check ka Gate banam Nifty50 aur Sensex ki suchi Tay nahi:** `[PRASTAAVIT-CC HAL]` `Breadth_Top_Stocks_Ginti`; tab tak Nifty50 aur Sensex sirf Paper Mode `[F-02]`.
- **[TAKRAAV #33] `[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Premium_Range_Gate banam Delta_Filter aur Balance_Ek_Lot_Gate:** Range unhi do se banti hai, isliye Gate dohraav hai aur purani range sahi strike rok sakti hai. `[PRASTAAVIT-CC HAL]` range har evaluation par bane; Range Alert ka matlab "paisa kam" `[F-24]`.
- **[TAKRAAV #34] `[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` `IV_Thanda_Paimana` banam IV Crush:** IV gir kar pehle se neeche aaye toh shart kabhi poori nahi hoti. `[PRASTAAVIT-CC HAL]` shart ek-taraf: IV daayre ke andar ya neeche `[F-37]`.
- **[TAKRAAV #35] `[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` 9 Nov ke Stage numbers aur Stage Override banam CC ke data-based Stage Gates:** `Paper_Mode_Avadhi`, `Paper_Mode_Trade_Ginti` aur `Stage2_Avadhi` chhote hain, aur Override se parakh chhoot sakti hai `[TAKRAAV #12]`. `[PRASTAAVIT-CC HAL]` `Paper_Gate_CC`, `Stage2_Gate_CC` aur Backtest Gate se pehle Real Money band `[F-18]` `[F-19]`.
- **[TAKRAAV #36] `[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Idea ke samay (3:15 PM square-off, 3:00 se 3:30 ka settlement ausat) banam exchange ke naye samay:** F&O ab 3:40 PM tak, aur 7 Dec 2026 se badlega. `[PRASTAAVIT-CC HAL]` bot roz exchange aur broker ki timing se milaye; malik naye samay tay kare (Sawal 137) `[F-63]`.
- **[TAKRAAV #37] `[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Pehredaar ka alag app banam "bina registration algo sirf ek API key se":** `[PRASTAAVIT-CC HAL]` Upstox aur vakil ki likhit pushti tak Real Money Mode band (Sawal 136) `[F-62]`.
- **[TAKRAAV #38] `[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Pehredaar ka alag Upstox app aur alag token (Section 5) banam broker ka "ek user, ek chalu API app" niyam (1 April 2026 se):** `[PRASTAAVIT-CC HAL]` ek hi app, roz ek hi token, login sirf ek jagah se; token surakshit raste se Pehredaar tak; Pehredaar Secondary IP se. Isse `[TAKRAAV #37]` bhi hal hota hai, agar Upstox shared token ki pushti kare (Sawal 141) `[F-76]`.

## 20. Tarikh wali baatein

Pehli baat (SEBI Retail Algo Niyam): Bot SEBI aur exchange ke retail algo niyam ke andar manjoor raaste se chalta hai. Yeh niyam 1 April 2026 se poori tarah lagu hain. Bot ek tay darj jagah se order bhejta hai, roz naya login karta hai aur algo pehchaan rakhta hai. Woh har second ke orders ki ginti ko `Order_Prati_Second_Seema` ke andar rakhta hai. Broker ke notice ke hisaab se Static IP ek ya do hote hain, aur har algo order par exchange ki di Algo-ID lagti hai. Roz ek baar 2FA zaroori hai aur lagatar chalne wala refresh-token login band hai. Regulator ki seema 10 orders prati second prati exchange prati client batayi gayi hai, jiske upar strategy darj karani padti hai. Kuch brokers ke hisaab se isme modify aur cancel bhi ginti mein aate hain. Market order MPP order mein badal jaata hai. Static IP hafte mein ek baar se zyada badalna mana hai (NSE ke standards ka saar). Kis tarikh tak sahi: 9 Nov 2026 (kisne batayi: Fyers, Shoonya aur Aliceblue ke notice va blog; SEBI ya NSE ka circular seedha nahi padha).

Doosri baat (Expiry ke Weekday aur Settlement): Nifty50 ki weekly expiry har Mangalwar ko aur Sensex ki har Guruwar ko hoti hai. BankNifty ki expiry mahine mein ek baar mahine ke aakhri Mangalwar ko hoti hai (chhutti par pichle din). Aakhri Mangalwar ko Nifty50, BankNifty aur saare bade stocks ke F&O contracts ek saath expire hote hain. Expiry par options ka final settlement `Expiry_Settlement_Ausat_Samay` (`3:00 se 3:30 PM`) ke ausat Spot Price par hota hai. Sensex ki monthly expiry mahine ke aakhri Guruwar ko hoti hai. November 2026 mein Nifty50 ki weekly expiry 10 Nov se hatkar 9 Nov (Somvaar) ko aayi, aur NSE ke monthly contracts 24 Nov se hatkar 23 Nov ko expire honge. Ek page par Sensex ka din Mangalwar likha mila, baaki saare srot Guruwar kehte hain. Settlement wali baat dobara jaanchi nahi. Kis tarikh tak sahi: 9 Nov 2026 (kisne batayi: algotest ka expiry calendar, share.market, Sahi aur onetradejournal; exchange ka circular seedha nahi padha).

Teesri baat (Lot, Freeze Limit aur Market-Stop): Ek Lot ki quantity aur freeze limit exchange samay-samay par tay karta hai. Exchange mein options par seedhe market-stop order band hain, isliye Broker Stop buffer wale limit bhav se lagta hai. Lot: Nifty50 mein 65, BankNifty mein 30 (January 2026 se) aur Sensex mein 20. Freeze Limit 2 Nov 2026 se Nifty50 mein 1,800 aur BankNifty mein 900 units hai (NSE circular NSE/FAOP/77189 ke hisaab se). 5 Oct 2026 wali badhi hui limit (Nifty50 3,510, BankNifty 1,440) ab hat chuki hai. Sensex ki freeze limit nahi mili. Market-stop wali baat dobara jaanchi nahi. Kis tarikh tak sahi: 9 Nov 2026 (kisne batayi: TradingQnA aur TeamLease RegTech ke circular saar, Sahi va Upstox ke lot-size notice; NSE ka circular seedha nahi padha).

Chauthi baat (Khaas Trading Din): Budget ka din ya Muhurat trading jaise khaas dino ki tarikh har saal alag hoti hai. Aise din bot bina malik ki manzoori ke apne aap normal niyam se trade nahi leta `[TAKRAAV #1]`. Kis tarikh tak sahi: 7 Nov 2026 (kisne batayi: user ki file).

Paanchvi baat (Jane Street Case): SEBI ne 3 July 2025 ke order mein 4,843.57 crore rupaye impound karne ko kaha tha. Case Securities Appellate Tribunal (SAT) mein chal raha hai. 21 Oct 2026 ko SAT ne Jane Street ki dastavez maangne wali arzi par order surakshit rakha, jo manipulation ka asli faisla nahi hai. Final faisla abhi nahi aaya hai aur Jane Street in aaropon ko nahi maanta. Bot kisi ko doshi nahi maanta balki isse Section 7 wale teen manipulation checks seekhta hai. Kis tarikh tak sahi: 9 Nov 2026 (kisne batayi: Business Standard 21 Oct 2026 aur LiveLaw).

Chhathi baat (Upstox API ki Seemayein, Token aur Square-Off): Upstox API mein standard order API ki seema 10 request prati second, 500 prati minute aur 2,000 prati 30 minute hai (multi-order API 4 prati second). Ek din mein ek order zyada se zyada 25 baar modify ho sakta hai. Upstox ka access token har din agle din subah 3:30 AM par khatam ho jaata hai aur koi refresh token nahi hota. Ek app ke liye naya token banate hi purana token band ho jaata hai, par ek account mein kai apps banaye ja sakte hain. Lambe samay wala extended token sirf padhne (read-only) ke kaam aata hai, usse order nahi lagte. Har order ke headers mein Algo Name bhejna hota hai. Upstox ke Intraday (MIS) auto square-off ke samay par do alag baatein mili: ek help page par 3:20 PM aur naye update page par Equity F&O ke liye 3:25 PM `[Sawal 114]`. Kis tarikh tak sahi: 9 Nov 2026 (kisne batayi: Upstox developer documentation, Upstox community forum aur Upstox help center).

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` **Dobara jaanch 10 Nov 2026 (Claude Code), primary source ke saath.** Upar ki 6 baatein nahi badli gayin; neeche sirf jaanch ka nateeja hai. Poora hisaab `docs/research/R_c_Compliance.md` aur `docs/research/R_d_Costs_Data.md` mein hai.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Pehli baat ki jaanch (SEBI Retail Algo Niyam): 1 April 2026 se poori tarah lagu hona sahi nikla. 10 orders prati second prati exchange tak exchange registration nahi chahiye; tab broker ek aam Algo-ID lagata hai. Static IP ek Primary aur ek Secondary, aur hafte mein ek baar se zyada nahi badal sakta. Naya: bina registration wala algo sirf ek pehle se tay API key se chal sakta hai (NSE standard A.4) `[F-62]`. Exchange par algo ka market order allowed nahi. Kis tarikh tak sahi: 10 Nov 2026. Kisne batayi: SEBI circular 4 Feb 2025 ([link](https://www.sebi.gov.in/legal/circulars/feb-2025/safer-participation-of-retail-investors-in-algorithmic-trading_91614.html)), SEBI circular 30 Sep 2025 ([PDF](https://www.sebi.gov.in/sebi_data/attachdocs/sep-2025/1759232056254.pdf)), NSE/INVG/67858 ([PDF](https://nsearchives.nseindia.com/content/circulars/INVG67858.pdf)). Verify nahi hua: modify aur cancel exchange ki 10 ki ginti mein aate hain ya nahi.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Doosri baat ki jaanch (Expiry aur Settlement): Nifty50 weekly Mangalwar, BankNifty sirf mahine ka aakhri Mangalwar, Sensex weekly Guruwar sahi nikle. Chhutti par expiry khisakti hai: NSE file mein Nifty50 ki expiry 9 Nov 2026, 23 Nov 2026 aur 25 Jan 2027 (teeno Somvaar) hai. `[F-63]` Badla: 3 Aug 2026 se F&O 3:40 PM tak chalta hai, aur 3 Aug se 6 Dec 2026 tak settlement "3:00 se 3:30 ka ausat" nahi hai. 7 Dec 2026 se index F&O 3:45 PM tak, par expiry din expire hone wale index contract 3:30 PM par band, aur settlement phir 3:00 se 3:30 ka ausat. Kis tarikh tak sahi: 10 Nov 2026. Kisne batayi: SEBI circular 16 Jan 2026 (CAS) aur SEBI circular 15 Oct 2026 ([link](https://www.sebi.gov.in/legal/circulars/oct-2026/review-of-certain-aspects-of-the-closing-auction-session-market-timings-and-settlement-methodology-f)), Upstox instrument file ([NSE](https://assets.upstox.com/market-quote/instruments/exchange/NSE.json.gz)).

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Teesri baat ki jaanch (Lot, Freeze Limit, Market-Stop): Lot Nifty50 mein 65, BankNifty mein 30, Sensex mein 20 sahi nikle. Badla: Upstox ki suchi mein Sensex ke January 2027 contracts ka Lot 25 hai (BSE ka notice verify nahi hua). Freeze Limit Nifty50 1,800 aur BankNifty 900 (NSE/FAOP/77189, 2 Nov 2026 se) sahi nikle, par Upstox ki file mein Nifty50 ka 1,755 dikha aur Sensex ka 1,000. Options par market-stop (SL-M) exchange 27 Sep 2021 se reject karta hai (NSE/FAOP/49677). Kis tarikh tak sahi: 10 Nov 2026. Kisne batayi: [NSE/FAOP/77189](https://nsearchives.nseindia.com/content/circulars/FAOP77189.pdf), [NSE/FAOP/49677](https://nsearchives.nseindia.com/content/circulars/FAOP49677.pdf), Upstox instrument file.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Chauthi baat ki jaanch (Khaas Trading Din): Muhurat trading 8 Nov 2026 (Ravivaar) ko tha. 2026 ki bachi F&O chhuttiyan: 24 Nov aur 25 Dec. Kis tarikh tak sahi: 10 Nov 2026. Kisne batayi: [NSE/FAOP/71777](https://nsearchives.nseindia.com/content/circulars/FAOP71777.pdf). Verify nahi hua: Budget 2027 ke din ka session.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Paanchvi baat ki jaanch (Jane Street Case): 3 July 2025 ka order aur 4,843.57 crore rupaye ka impound sahi nikla. 21 July 2025 ko escrow ke baad trading ki rok hati (secondary source). 21 Oct 2026 ko SAT ne dastavez wali arzi par order surakshit rakha; yeh final faisla nahi. 10 Nov 2026 tak koi nayi khabar nahi mili (verify nahi hua). Kisne batayi: SEBI order 3 July 2025, Business Standard 8 Oct 2026.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Chhathi baat ki jaanch (Upstox): order API ki seema 10 prati second, 500 prati minute, 2,000 prati 30 minute sahi nikli; multi-order ka alag "4 prati second" ab docs mein nahi, ek hi milee-juli seema hai. Token agle din 3:30 AM tak, naya token purana band karta hai, extended token sirf padhne ke liye: sahi. `[F-71]` "Ek order 25 baar modify" Upstox docs mein nahi mila (verify nahi hua). Square-off ka takraav hal: F&O ka auto square-off 3:25 PM (4 Sep 2026 se), 3:24 PM ke baad naya intraday order nahi, aur broker ke square-off par charge lagta hai `[Sawal 114]`. `[F-68]` Algo Name header docs ke hisaab se sirf exchange-approved algo ke liye zaroori hai. Kisne batayi: [rate limits](https://upstox.com/developer/api-documentation/rate-limiting/), [static IP](https://upstox.com/developer/api-documentation/announcements/algo-trading-circular/), [place order](https://upstox.com/developer/api-documentation/v3/place-order/), [token](https://upstox.com/developer/api-documentation/get-token/), [square-off](https://upstox.com/announcements/revised-timings/important-update-intraday-square-off-timings-are-changing/).

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Saatvi baat (nayi, Upstox ke aur niyam): Static IP badalte hi purane token band hote hain `[F-65]`. Live data mein ek account par 2 connection milte hain, aur "full" mode mein 2,000 instrument tak `[F-69]`. Order aur position ki khabar ke liye alag portfolio stream hai, jo doosre platform ke order bhi dikhata hai. Sandbox sirf order ka dhaancha jaanchta hai `[F-67]`. Broker ka Kill Switch hai, par pehle positions band karni padti hain `[F-66]`. Login ke liye official TOTP rasta bhi ab hai (Sawal 113). Kis tarikh tak sahi: 10 Nov 2026. Kisne batayi: [feed](https://upstox.com/developer/api-documentation/v3/get-market-data-feed/), [portfolio stream](https://upstox.com/developer/api-documentation/get-portfolio-stream-feed/), [sandbox](https://upstox.com/developer/api-documentation/sandbox/), [kill switch](https://upstox.com/developer/api-documentation/announcements/kill-switch-api/), [IP update](https://upstox.com/developer/api-documentation/update-app-static-ips/).

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Aathvi baat (nayi, kharche): option bechne par STT 1 April 2026 se premium ka 0.15 percent hai (pehle 0.10). NSE index options ka transaction charge 1 March 2026 se 0.03553 percent hai. Upstox brokerage 20 rupaye prati executed order hai, aur API order par 10 rupaye ka offer "31 Dec 2026 tak" likha hai (do pages mein tarikh alag). Isse Nifty50 ke 1 Lot ka round-trip kharcha lagbhag 80 rupaye banta hai, Slippage alag. Kis tarikh tak sahi: 10 Nov 2026. Kisne batayi: NSE circular NSE/FATAX/73524, NSE/FA/73061, Upstox brokerage page; hisaab `docs/research/R_d_Costs_Data.md` mein.

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Naveen baat (nayi, retail nuksan): SEBI ki Aug 2026 study ke hisaab se FY26 mein 87.7 percent individual F&O traders ko nuksan hua, kul nuksan 91,685 crore rupaye. Option buyers mein lagbhag 90 percent nuksan mein the. Pehle ki study (Sep 2024) mein FY22 se FY24 ke beech 93 percent ko nuksan hua tha. Kis tarikh tak sahi: 10 Nov 2026. Kisne batayi: [SEBI study FY25-FY26](https://www.sebi.gov.in/sebi_data/attachdocs/aug-2026/1787233506209.pdf), [SEBI trading behaviour study](https://www.sebi.gov.in/sebi_data/attachdocs/aug-2026/1787233601328.pdf).

`[PRASTAAVIT-CC]` Dasvi baat (nayi, ek user ek app): Upstox ki team ne 27 Mar 2026 ko likha: "From 1 April, only a single active API app per user will be allowed". Sabse naya bana app chalu rehta hai, baaki apps delete ho jaate hain. Static IP API se trading ke liye zaroori hai. Token ke baare mein yeh post kuch nahi kehta. Kis tarikh tak sahi: 10 Nov 2026 (post 27 Mar 2026 ka). Kisne batayi: Upstox community par Team Upstox ka post ([link](https://community.upstox.com/t/important-new-sebi-exchange-mandates-for-api-trading-effective-1st-april-2026/14822)); yeh official docs page nahi, isliye Upstox support se likhit pushti baaki `[F-76]`.
